  182|       |    // Check for pops doing things
  183|      0|    let mut moving = 0;

  204|      0|            _ => {}
  205|       |        }
  206|      0|        if assigned.is_some() {
  207|      0|            // Already counted above
  208|      0|        }

  271|      0|fn print_map(world: &mut World, center_x: i32, center_y: i32) {
  272|      0|    let radius = 10;
  273|       |
  274|       |    // Copy terrain data before querying to avoid borrow conflicts

  279|       |
  280|      0|        let mut tiles = std::collections::HashMap::new();
  281|      0|        for y in (center_y - radius)..=(center_y + radius) {
  282|      0|            for x in (center_x - radius)..=(center_x + radius) {
  283|      0|                if x >= 0
  284|      0|                    && y >= 0
  285|      0|                    && x < max_x
  286|      0|                    && y < max_y

  288|      0|                {
  289|      0|                    tiles.insert((x, y), t);
  290|      0|                }
  291|       |            }
  292|       |        }
  293|      0|        (max_x, max_y, tiles)
  294|       |    };
  295|       |
  296|      0|    println!("=== Map around ({center_x}, {center_y}) ===");
  297|       |

  300|      0|        .query::<&GridPosition>()

  302|      0|        .map(|p| (p.x, p.y))
  303|      0|        .collect();
  304|       |

  306|      0|    let designation_positions: Vec<(i32, i32, DesignationType)> = world
  307|      0|        .query::<(&GridPosition, &Designation)>()

  313|      0|        print!("{y:3} ");

  315|      0|            if x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= width || y >= height {
  316|      0|                print!(" ");
  317|      0|                continue;
  318|      0|            }
  319|       |
  320|       |            // Check for pop
  321|      0|            if pop_positions.iter().any(|&(px, py)| px == x && py == y) {
  322|      0|                print!("@");
  323|      0|                continue;
  324|      0|            }
  325|       |
  326|       |            // Check for designation
  327|      0|            if let Some((_, _, dt)) = designation_positions
  328|      0|                .iter()
  329|      0|                .find(|&&(dx, dy, _)| dx == x && dy == y)
  330|       |            {
  331|      0|                let c = match dt {
  332|      0|                    DesignationType::Mine => '%',
  333|      0|                    DesignationType::Chop => '/',
  334|      0|                    DesignationType::Demolish => 'X',
  335|      0|                    DesignationType::Repair => '+',
  336|       |                };
  337|      0|                print!("{c}");
  338|      0|                continue;
  339|      0|            }
  340|       |
  341|       |            // Show terrain
  342|      0|            let tile = terrain_tiles
  343|      0|                .get(&(x, y))
  344|      0|                .copied()
  345|      0|                .unwrap_or(TerrainType::Grass);
  346|      0|            let c = match tile {
  347|      0|                TerrainType::Grass => '.',
  348|      0|                TerrainType::Dirt => ',',
  349|      0|                TerrainType::Rock => '#',
  350|      0|                TerrainType::Water => '~',
  351|      0|                TerrainType::Tree => 'T',
  352|       |            };
  353|      0|            print!("{c}");
  354|       |        }
  355|      0|        println!();
  356|       |    }
  357|      0|    println!("Legend: @=pop .=grass ,=dirt #=rock ~=water T=tree %=mine /=chop");

  360|      0|fn build_at(world: &mut World, building_type: BuildingType, x: i32, y: i32) {

  500|       |        }
  501|      0|        (max_x, max_y, tiles)

  534|      0|                DesignationType::Repair => "repair",

  537|      0|        })

  543|      0|                continue;

    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! Tracks significant life events for individual pops.
    4|       |
    5|       |use crate::layer1::actions::{AssignedTo, AssignmentType};

    7|       |use crate::layer1::pop::Pop;

   10|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   11|       |
   12|       |/// A single event in a Pop's life.
   13|       |#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
   14|       |pub struct BiographyEvent {
   15|       |    /// The tick when the event occurred.
   16|       |    pub tick: u64,
   17|       |    /// The text description of the event.
   18|       |    pub text: String,
   19|       |}
   20|       |
   21|       |/// Component storing the history of a Pop.
   22|       |#[derive(Component, Default, Debug, Clone)]
   23|       |pub struct Biography {
   24|       |    /// List of life events.
   25|       |    pub events: Vec<BiographyEvent>,
   26|       |}
   27|       |
   28|       |impl Biography {
   29|       |    /// Add a new event to the biography.
   30|      2|    pub fn add_event(&mut self, tick: u64, text: String) {
   31|      2|        self.events.push(BiographyEvent { tick, text });
   32|      2|    }
   33|       |}
   34|       |
   35|       |/// System to monitor and record biography events.
   36|     15|pub fn biography_monitor_system(
   37|     15|    time: Res<SimulationTime>,
   38|     15|    new_pops: Query<Entity, (With<Pop>, Without<Biography>)>,
   39|     15|    mut bio_pops: Query<(Entity, &mut Biography, &AssignedTo)>,
   40|     15|    buildings: Query<&Building>,
   41|     15|    mut commands: Commands,
   42|     15|    generator: Res<NarrativeGenerator>,
   43|     15|) {
   44|     15|    let current_tick = time.tick;
   45|       |
   46|       |    // 1. Initialize new Pops
   47|     31|    for entity in &new_pops {
                      ^16
   48|     16|        let arrival = generator
   49|     16|            .get_random_fragment("POP_ARRIVAL_METHOD")
   50|     16|            .map_or_else(
   51|      0|                || "Joined the colony.".to_string(),
   52|     16|                |method| format!("Arrived {method}. Joined the colony."),
   53|       |            );
   54|     16|        commands.entity(entity).insert(Biography {
   55|     16|            events: vec![BiographyEvent {
   56|     16|                tick: current_tick,
   57|     16|                text: arrival,
   58|     16|            }],
   59|     16|        });
   60|       |    }
   61|       |
   62|       |    // 2. Monitor Assignments
   63|     17|    for (_, mut bio, assigned) in &mut bio_pops {
                          ^2       ^2
   64|      2|        let target_name = buildings
   65|      2|            .get(assigned.entity)
   66|      2|            .map_or("Unknown Building", |building| {
   67|      2|                building.building_type.label()
   68|      2|            });
   69|       |
   70|      2|        let event_text = match assigned.assignment_type {
   71|      2|            AssignmentType::FarmWorker => format!("Started working at {target_name}."),
   72|      0|            AssignmentType::HousingResident => format!("Moved into {target_name}."),
   73|      0|            AssignmentType::TavernVisitor => format!("Visited {target_name} to socialize."),
   74|      0|            AssignmentType::LibraryWorker => format!("Started research at {target_name}."),
   75|       |        };
   76|       |
   77|       |        // Avoid duplicate consecutive events
   78|      2|        if let Some(last) = bio.events.last()
                                  ^1
   79|      1|            && last.text == event_text
   80|       |        {
   81|      1|            continue;
   82|      1|        }
   83|       |
   84|      1|        bio.add_event(current_tick, event_text);
   85|       |    }
   86|     15|}

  196|      1|            inputs.push(GpuBuildingInput {
  197|      1|                pos_x: pos.x,
  198|      1|                pos_y: pos.y,
  199|      1|                building_type: 2,
  200|      1|                capacity: tavern.capacity as u32,
  201|      1|                occupied: tavern.visitors.len() as u32,
  202|      1|                resource_has_room: 0,
  203|      1|                _padding: [0; 2],
  204|      1|            });
  205|      1|        }
  206|       |    }
  207|       |
  208|       |    // Libraries (building_type = 3)
  209|       |    {
  210|     10|        let mut query = world.query::<(Entity, &GridPosition, &Library)>();
  211|     10|        for (entity, pos, _library) in query.iter(world) {
                           ^1      ^1   ^1
  212|      1|            entities.push(entity);
  213|      1|            inputs.push(GpuBuildingInput {
  214|      1|                pos_x: pos.x,
  215|      1|                pos_y: pos.y,
  216|      1|                building_type: 3,
  217|      1|                capacity: 5,
  218|      1|                occupied: 0,
  219|      1|                resource_has_room: 0,
  220|      1|                _padding: [0; 2],
  221|      1|            });
  222|      1|        }
  223|       |    }
  224|       |

  226|       |    {
  227|       |        use crate::layer1::designation::DesignationType;
  228|     10|        let mut query = world.query::<(Entity, &GridPosition, &Designation)>();
  229|     10|        for (entity, pos, designation) in query.iter(world) {
                           ^1      ^1   ^1

  231|      0|                6
  232|       |            } else {
  233|      1|                4
  234|       |            };
  235|      1|            entities.push(entity);
  236|      1|            inputs.push(GpuBuildingInput {
  237|      1|                pos_x: pos.x,
  238|      1|                pos_y: pos.y,
  239|      1|                building_type,
  240|      1|                capacity: 1,
  241|      1|                occupied: 0,
  242|      1|                resource_has_room: 0,
  243|      1|                _padding: [0; 2],
  244|      1|            });
  245|       |        }
  246|       |    }
  247|       |
  248|       |    // ResourceItems (building_type = 5)
  249|       |    {
  250|     10|        let resources = world
  251|     10|            .get_resource::<ColonyResources>()
  252|     10|            .cloned()
  253|     10|            .unwrap_or_default();
  254|       |
  255|     10|        let mut query = world.query::<(Entity, &GridPosition, &ResourceItem)>();
  256|     10|        for (entity, pos, item) in query.iter(world) {
                           ^1      ^1   ^1
  257|      1|            let has_room = match item.resource_type {
  258|      0|                ResourceType::Food => resources.food < resources.max_food,
  259|      0|                ResourceType::Wood => resources.wood < resources.max_wood,
  260|      1|                ResourceType::Stone => resources.stone < resources.max_stone,
  261|      0|                ResourceType::Ore => resources.ore < resources.max_ore,
  262|      0|                ResourceType::Metal => resources.metal < resources.max_metal,
  263|      0|                ResourceType::Planks => resources.planks < resources.max_planks,
  264|      0|                ResourceType::Blocks => resources.blocks < resources.max_blocks,
  265|       |            };
  266|       |
  267|      1|            entities.push(entity);
  268|      1|            inputs.push(GpuBuildingInput {
  269|      1|                pos_x: pos.x,
  270|      1|                pos_y: pos.y,
  271|      1|                building_type: 5,
  272|      1|                capacity: 1,
  273|      1|                occupied: 0,
  274|      1|                resource_has_room: u32::from(has_room),
  275|      1|                _padding: [0; 2],
  276|      1|            });
  277|       |        }
  278|       |    }
  279|       |
  280|     10|    (entities, inputs)
  281|     10|}

  283|       |/// Extracts global state for GPU evaluation.
  284|     10|pub fn extract_global_state(
  285|     10|    world: &mut World,
  286|     10|    pop_count: u32,
  287|     10|    building_count: u32,
  288|     10|) -> GpuGlobalState {
  289|     10|    let switch_threshold = world
  290|     10|        .get_resource::<UtilityConfig>()
  291|     10|        .map_or(0.15, |c| c.switch_threshold);
  292|       |
  293|     10|    let resources = world
  294|     10|        .get_resource::<ColonyResources>()
  295|     10|        .cloned()
  296|     10|        .unwrap_or_default();
  297|       |
  298|     10|    let knowledge_full = u32::from(resources.knowledge >= resources.max_knowledge);
  299|       |
  300|     10|    let mut has_stockpile_query = world.query::<&Stockpile>();
  301|     10|    let has_stockpile = u32::from(has_stockpile_query.iter(world).next().is_some());
  302|       |
  303|     10|    GpuGlobalState {
  304|     10|        switch_threshold,
  305|     10|        knowledge_full,
  306|     10|        has_stockpile,
  307|     10|        pop_count,
  308|     10|        building_count,
  309|     10|        _padding: [0; 3],
  310|     10|    }

/app/src/gpu/context.rs:
    1|       |#![allow(unsafe_code)]
    2|       |//! wgpu device and queue initialization.
    3|       |
    4|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
    5|       |
    6|       |use super::pipeline::create_compute_pipeline;
    7|       |
    8|       |/// Holds all GPU state needed for compute dispatch.
    9|       |pub struct GpuContext {
   10|       |    /// The wgpu logical device.
   11|       |    pub device: wgpu::Device,
   12|       |    /// The command queue.
   13|       |    pub queue: wgpu::Queue,
   14|       |    /// The compiled compute pipeline.
   15|       |    pub pipeline: wgpu::ComputePipeline,
   16|       |    /// The bind group layout for the compute shader.
   17|       |    pub bind_group_layout: wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
   18|       |}
   19|       |
   20|       |// On native, wgpu types are Send + Sync. On wasm32 they wrap JS objects
   21|       |// and are !Send + !Sync, but WASM is single-threaded so this is safe.
   22|       |#[cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]
   23|       |// SAFETY: WASM is single-threaded; these types will never be sent across threads.
   24|       |unsafe impl Send for GpuContext {}
   25|       |#[cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]
   26|       |// SAFETY: WASM is single-threaded; these types will never be accessed from multiple threads.
   27|       |unsafe impl Sync for GpuContext {}
   28|       |
   29|       |impl Resource for GpuContext {}
   30|       |
   31|       |impl GpuContext {
   32|       |    /// Initialize GPU context: adapter, device, queue, pipeline.
   33|       |    ///
   34|       |    /// This is async because wgpu initialization is async.
   35|       |    /// Use `pollster::block_on(GpuContext::new())` for synchronous init.
   36|       |    ///
   37|       |    /// # Errors
   38|       |    ///
   39|       |    /// Returns an error if no suitable GPU adapter is found or device
   40|       |    /// creation fails.
   41|      8|    pub async fn new() -> anyhow::Result<Self> {
   42|      8|        let instance = wgpu::Instance::new(&wgpu::InstanceDescriptor {
   43|      8|            backends: wgpu::Backends::all(),
   44|      8|            ..Default::default()
   45|      8|        });
   46|       |
   47|      8|        let adapter = instance
   48|      8|            .request_adapter(&wgpu::RequestAdapterOptions {
   49|      8|                power_preference: wgpu::PowerPreference::HighPerformance,
   50|      8|                force_fallback_adapter: false,
   51|      8|                compatible_surface: None,
   52|      8|            })
   53|      8|            .await
   54|      8|            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("No suitable GPU adapter found"))?;
                                         ^0              ^0                               ^0
   55|       |
   56|      8|        let (device, queue) = adapter
   57|      8|            .request_device(
   58|      8|                &wgpu::DeviceDescriptor {
   59|      8|                    label: Some("scale-gpu"),
   60|      8|                    required_features: wgpu::Features::empty(),
   61|      8|                    required_limits: wgpu::Limits::default(),
   62|      8|                    memory_hints: wgpu::MemoryHints::default(),
   63|      8|                },
   64|      8|                None,
   65|      8|            )
   66|      8|            .await
   67|      8|            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("{e}"))?;
                                       ^0              ^0     ^0
   68|       |
   69|      8|        let (pipeline, bind_group_layout) = create_compute_pipeline(&device);
   70|       |
   71|      8|        Ok(Self {
   72|      8|            device,
   73|      8|            queue,
   74|      8|            pipeline,
   75|      8|            bind_group_layout,
   76|      8|        })
   77|      8|    }
   78|       |}
   79|       |
   80|       |#[cfg(test)]
   81|       |mod tests {
   82|       |    use super::*;
   83|       |    use std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe;
   84|       |
   85|       |    #[test]
   86|      1|    fn test_gpu_context_creation() {
   87|       |        // This test requires a GPU and a valid shader. Skip gracefully if
   88|       |        // unavailable or if shader validation fails (which panics in wgpu).
   89|      1|        let result =
   90|      1|            std::panic::catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| pollster::block_on(GpuContext::new())));
   91|       |
   92|      1|        match result {
   93|      1|            Ok(Ok(ctx)) => {
   94|       |                // Basic sanity -- device and queue exist, pipeline created.
   95|      1|                assert!(ctx.device.limits().max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension > 0);
   96|       |            }
   97|      0|            Ok(Err(e)) => {
   98|      0|                eprintln!("Skipping GPU test (no adapter): {e}");
   99|      0|            }
  100|      0|            Err(_) => {
  101|      0|                eprintln!("Skipping GPU test (shader validation panic)");
  102|      0|            }
  103|       |        }
  104|      1|    }
  105|       |}

/app/src/gpu/evaluate.rs:
    1|       |//! GPU-based replacement for `evaluate_actions_system`.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! Orchestrates: extract ECS data → upload to GPU → dispatch compute → readback → apply to ECS.
    4|       |
    5|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
    6|       |use wgpu::util::DeviceExt;
    7|       |
    8|       |use super::buffers::{
    9|       |    GpuPopDecision, extract_building_inputs, extract_global_state, extract_pop_inputs,
   10|       |};
   11|       |use super::context::GpuContext;
   12|       |use crate::layer1::utility_ai::evaluate_actions_system;
   13|       |use crate::layer1::utility_ai::types::{ActionType, PopAction, StartPlan};
   14|       |
   15|       |/// GPU-accelerated action evaluation system.
   16|       |///
   17|       |/// Drop-in replacement for `evaluate_actions_system` with signature `fn(&mut World)`.
   18|       |/// Falls back to the CPU `evaluate_actions_system` if `GpuContext` is not available.
   19|       |#[allow(clippy::cast_possible_truncation)]
   20|     14|pub fn gpu_evaluate_actions(world: &mut World) {
   21|       |    // Fall back to CPU path if no GPU context
   22|     14|    if !world.contains_resource::<GpuContext>() {
   23|      2|        evaluate_actions_system(world);
   24|      2|        return;
   25|     12|    }
   26|       |
   27|       |    // 1. Extract data from ECS
   28|     12|    let (pop_entities, pop_inputs) = extract_pop_inputs(world);
   29|     12|    if pop_inputs.is_empty() {
   30|      3|        return;
   31|      9|    }
   32|       |
   33|      9|    let (building_entities, building_inputs) = extract_building_inputs(world);
   34|       |
   35|      9|    let global_state =
   36|      9|        extract_global_state(world, pop_inputs.len() as u32, building_inputs.len() as u32);
   37|       |
   38|       |    // 2. Get GPU context
   39|      9|    let gpu = world.resource::<GpuContext>();
   40|       |
   41|       |    // 3. Dispatch and readback
   42|      9|    let decisions = dispatch_and_readback(gpu, &pop_inputs, &building_inputs, &global_state);
   43|       |
   44|      9|    let Some(decisions) = decisions else {
   45|      0|        return;
   46|       |    };
   47|       |
   48|       |    // 4. Apply decisions to ECS
   49|      9|    apply_decisions(world, &pop_entities, &building_entities, &decisions);
   50|     14|}
   51|       |
   52|       |/// Upload data, dispatch the compute shader, and read back results.

   55|      9|    gpu: &GpuContext,
   56|      9|    pop_inputs: &[super::buffers::GpuPopInput],
   57|      9|    building_inputs: &[super::buffers::GpuBuildingInput],

   65|      9|            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE,
   66|      9|        });
   67|       |
   68|      9|    let building_buffer = if building_inputs.is_empty() {
   69|       |        // wgpu requires non-zero size buffers for storage bindings.
   70|      9|        gpu.device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
   71|      9|            label: Some("building-input-buffer-empty"),

   73|      9|            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE,
   74|      9|            mapped_at_creation: false,
   75|      9|        })
   76|       |    } else {
   77|      0|        gpu.device
   78|      0|            .create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
   79|      0|                label: Some("building-input-buffer"),
   80|      0|                contents: bytemuck::cast_slice(building_inputs),
   81|      0|                usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE,
   82|      0|            })
   83|       |    };
   84|       |

   86|      9|        .device

   88|      9|            label: Some("global-state-buffer"),
   89|      9|            contents: bytemuck::bytes_of(global_state),
   90|      9|            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM,
   91|      9|        });
   92|       |
   93|      9|    let decision_size =
   94|      9|        (pop_inputs.len() * std::mem::size_of::<GpuPopDecision>()) as wgpu::BufferAddress;
   95|       |
   96|      9|    let decision_buffer = gpu.device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
   97|      9|        label: Some("decision-output-buffer"),

  100|      9|        mapped_at_creation: false,
  101|      9|    });

  104|      9|        label: Some("decision-staging-buffer"),
  105|      9|        size: decision_size,
  106|      9|        usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
  107|      9|        mapped_at_creation: false,
  108|      9|    });
  109|       |
  110|      9|    let bind_group = gpu.device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
  111|      9|        label: Some("utility-ai-bind-group"),
  112|      9|        layout: &gpu.bind_group_layout,
  113|      9|        entries: &[
  114|      9|            wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
  115|      9|                binding: 0,
  116|      9|                resource: pop_buffer.as_entire_binding(),
  117|      9|            },
  118|      9|            wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
  119|      9|                binding: 1,
  120|      9|                resource: building_buffer.as_entire_binding(),
  121|      9|            },
  122|      9|            wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
  123|      9|                binding: 2,
  124|      9|                resource: global_buffer.as_entire_binding(),
  125|      9|            },
  126|      9|            wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
  127|      9|                binding: 3,
  128|      9|                resource: decision_buffer.as_entire_binding(),
  129|      9|            },
  130|      9|        ],
  131|      9|    });
  132|       |
  133|      9|    let workgroup_count = (pop_inputs.len() as u32).div_ceil(64);
  134|       |
  135|      9|    let mut encoder = gpu
  136|      9|        .device
  137|      9|        .create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
  138|      9|            label: Some("utility-ai-encoder"),
  139|      9|        });
  140|       |
  141|      9|    {
  142|      9|        let mut pass = encoder.begin_compute_pass(&wgpu::ComputePassDescriptor {
  143|      9|            label: Some("utility-ai-pass"),
  144|      9|            timestamp_writes: None,
  145|      9|        });
  146|      9|        pass.set_pipeline(&gpu.pipeline);
  147|      9|        pass.set_bind_group(0, &bind_group, &[]);
  148|      9|        pass.dispatch_workgroups(workgroup_count, 1, 1);
  149|      9|    }
  150|       |
  151|      9|    encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(&decision_buffer, 0, &staging_buffer, 0, decision_size);
  152|      9|    gpu.queue.submit(std::iter::once(encoder.finish()));
  153|       |
  154|       |    // Blocking readback
  155|      9|    let buffer_slice = staging_buffer.slice(..);
  156|      9|    let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
  157|      9|    buffer_slice.map_async(wgpu::MapMode::Read, move |result| {
  158|      9|        let _ = tx.send(result);
  159|      9|    });
  160|      9|    gpu.device.poll(wgpu::Maintain::Wait);
  161|       |
  162|      9|    rx.recv().ok()?.ok()?;
                                ^0    ^0
  163|       |
  164|      9|    let data = buffer_slice.get_mapped_range();
  165|      9|    let decisions: Vec<GpuPopDecision> = bytemuck::cast_slice(&data).to_vec();
  166|      9|    drop(data);
  167|      9|    staging_buffer.unmap();
  168|       |
  169|      9|    Some(decisions)
  170|      9|}
  171|       |
  172|       |/// Apply GPU decisions back to the ECS world.
  173|      9|fn apply_decisions(
  174|      9|    world: &mut World,
  175|      9|    pop_entities: &[Entity],
  176|      9|    building_entities: &[Entity],
  177|      9|    decisions: &[GpuPopDecision],
  178|      9|) {
  179|     45|    for (i, decision) in decisions.iter().enumerate() {
                                       ^9        ^9     ^9
  180|     45|        if decision.switched != 1 {
  181|     45|            continue;
  182|      0|        }
  183|       |
  184|      0|        let pop_entity = pop_entities[i];
  185|      0|        let action_type = action_type_from_u32(decision.best_action);
  186|       |
  187|      0|        if let Some(mut pop_action) = world.get_mut::<PopAction>(pop_entity) {
  188|      0|            pop_action.current = action_type;
  189|      0|            pop_action.current_utility = decision.best_utility;
  190|      0|            pop_action.ticks_committed = 0;
  191|      0|        }
  192|       |
  193|      0|        let target = if decision.target_index == u32::MAX {
  194|      0|            None
  195|       |        } else {
  196|       |            #[allow(clippy::cast_possible_truncation)]
  197|      0|            building_entities
  198|      0|                .get(decision.target_index as usize)
  199|      0|                .copied()
  200|       |        };
  201|       |
  202|      0|        world.entity_mut(pop_entity).insert(StartPlan {
  203|      0|            action: action_type,
  204|      0|            target,
  205|      0|        });
  206|       |    }

  209|       |/// Maps a u32 action index back to an `ActionType`.
  210|      9|const fn action_type_from_u32(v: u32) -> ActionType {
  211|      9|    match v {
  212|      1|        0 => ActionType::SatisfyHunger,
  213|      1|        1 => ActionType::SatisfyRest,
  214|      1|        2 => ActionType::Socialize,
  215|      1|        3 => ActionType::Explore,

  217|      1|        5 => ActionType::Research,
  218|      1|        6 => ActionType::Haul,
  219|      2|        _ => ActionType::Idle,
  220|       |    }

    1|       |//! Compute pipeline creation and bind group layout.
    2|       |
    3|       |/// Creates the compute pipeline and bind group layout for utility AI evaluation.
    4|       |///
    5|       |/// Bind group layout (group 0):
    6|       |/// - binding 0: `pops` -- storage buffer (read-only)
    7|       |/// - binding 1: `buildings` -- storage buffer (read-only)
    8|       |/// - binding 2: `globals` -- uniform buffer
    9|       |/// - binding 3: `decisions` -- storage buffer (read-write)
   10|       |#[must_use]
   11|      9|pub fn create_compute_pipeline(
   12|      9|    device: &wgpu::Device,
   13|      9|) -> (wgpu::ComputePipeline, wgpu::BindGroupLayout) {

   17|      9|        source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(shader_source.into()),
   18|      9|    });
   19|       |

   21|      9|        label: Some("utility-ai-bind-group-layout"),
   22|      9|        entries: &[
   23|      9|            // binding 0: pops (storage, read-only)
   24|      9|            wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
   25|      9|                binding: 0,
   26|      9|                visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::COMPUTE,
   27|      9|                ty: wgpu::BindingType::Buffer {
   28|      9|                    ty: wgpu::BufferBindingType::Storage { read_only: true },
   29|      9|                    has_dynamic_offset: false,
   30|      9|                    min_binding_size: None,
   31|      9|                },
   32|      9|                count: None,
   33|      9|            },
   34|      9|            // binding 1: buildings (storage, read-only)
   35|      9|            wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
   36|      9|                binding: 1,
   37|      9|                visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::COMPUTE,
   38|      9|                ty: wgpu::BindingType::Buffer {
   39|      9|                    ty: wgpu::BufferBindingType::Storage { read_only: true },
   40|      9|                    has_dynamic_offset: false,
   41|      9|                    min_binding_size: None,
   42|      9|                },
   43|      9|                count: None,
   44|      9|            },

   58|      9|                binding: 3,

   61|      9|                    ty: wgpu::BufferBindingType::Storage { read_only: false },
   62|      9|                    has_dynamic_offset: false,
   63|      9|                    min_binding_size: None,
   64|      9|                },
   65|      9|                count: None,
   66|      9|            },
   67|      9|        ],
   68|      9|    });
   69|       |
   70|      9|    let pipeline_layout = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
   71|      9|        label: Some("utility-ai-pipeline-layout"),
   72|      9|        bind_group_layouts: &[&bind_group_layout],
   73|      9|        push_constant_ranges: &[],
   74|      9|    });
   75|       |

   77|      9|        label: Some("utility-ai-compute-pipeline"),
   78|      9|        layout: Some(&pipeline_layout),
   79|      9|        module: &shader_module,
   80|      9|        entry_point: Some("main"),
   81|      9|        compilation_options: wgpu::PipelineCompilationOptions::default(),
   82|      9|        cache: None,
   83|      9|    });
   84|       |
   85|      9|    (pipeline, bind_group_layout)
   86|      9|}

  294|       |
  295|       |/// Attempt to place a building at the given position.
  296|       |/// Returns true if successful, false if placement blocked.
  297|       |///
  298|       |/// This will:
  299|       |/// 1. Check `can_place_building` (bounds, terrain, occupation).
  300|       |/// 2. Spawn a building entity with the correct components (e.g., `Housing` or `Farm`).
  301|       |/// 3. Mark the tile as occupied in `OccupiedTiles`.
  302|       |///
  303|       |/// # Examples
  304|       |///
  305|       |/// ```
  306|       |/// use scale::layer1::building::{try_place_building, BuildingType, OccupiedTiles};
  307|       |/// use scale::layer1::resources::ColonyResources;
  308|       |/// use scale::layer1::terrain::{TerrainGrid, TerrainType};
  309|       |/// use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
  310|       |///
  311|       |/// let mut world = World::new();
  312|       |/// let tiles = vec![TerrainType::Grass; 100]; // 10x10 grass
  313|       |/// world.insert_resource(TerrainGrid { width: 10, height: 10, tiles });
  314|       |/// world.insert_resource(OccupiedTiles::default());
  315|       |/// world.insert_resource(ColonyResources {
  316|       |///     wood: 100.0,
  317|       |///     ..Default::default()
  318|       |/// });
  319|       |///
  320|       |/// let placed = try_place_building(&mut world, 5, 5, BuildingType::Housing);
  321|       |/// assert!(placed);
  322|       |/// ```
  323|     15|pub fn try_place_building(world: &mut World, x: i32, y: i32, building_type: BuildingType) -> bool {
  324|     15|    if let Err(e) = validate_building_placement(world, x, y) {
                             ^3
  325|      3|        handle_placement_error(world, e);
  326|      3|        return false;
  327|     12|    }
  328|       |
  329|       |    // Check Tech requirements
  330|     12|    if let Some(tech) = building_type.required_tech() {
                              ^2
  331|       |        // We use get_resource because TechState might not be initialized in some tests
  332|       |        // (though we should initialize it)
  333|       |        // If it's missing, we default to "locked" to be safe.
  334|      2|        let tech_unlocked = world
  335|      2|            .get_resource::<TechState>()
  336|      2|            .is_some_and(|state| state.is_unlocked(tech));
  337|       |
  338|      2|        if !tech_unlocked {
  339|      1|            if let Some(mut log) = world.get_resource_mut::<MessageLog>() {
  340|      1|                log.add(format!("Requires technology: {}", tech.label()));
  341|      1|            }
                          ^0
  342|      1|            return false;
  343|      1|        }
  344|     10|    }
  345|       |
  346|       |    // Check affordability
  347|     11|    let cost = building_type.cost();
  348|     11|    let can_afford = {
  349|     11|        let resources = world.resource::<ColonyResources>();
  350|     11|        resources.can_afford(&cost)
  351|       |    };
  352|       |
  353|     11|    if !can_afford {
  354|      1|        if let Some(mut log) = world.get_resource_mut::<MessageLog>() {
                                  ^0
  355|      0|            log.add(format!(
  356|      0|                "Not enough resources for {}",
  357|      0|                building_type.label()
  358|      0|            ));
  359|      1|        }
  360|      1|        return false;
  361|     10|    }
  362|       |
  363|       |    // Deduct cost
  364|     10|    world.resource_mut::<ColonyResources>().deduct(&cost);
  365|       |
  366|       |    // Spawn building
  367|     10|    spawn_building(world, x, y, building_type);
  368|       |
  369|       |    // Mark tile occupied
  370|     10|    world.resource_mut::<OccupiedTiles>().0.insert((x, y));
  371|       |
  372|     10|    if let Some(mut log) = world.get_resource_mut::<MessageLog>() {
                              ^2
  373|      2|        log.add(format!("Construction started: {}", building_type.label()));
  374|      8|    }
  375|       |
  376|     10|    true
  377|     15|}

/app/src/layer1/chronicle.rs:
    1|       |//! Chronicle system and historical records.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! The Chronicle is the "Memory" of the colony. It does not just log debug messages;
    4|       |//! it records significant events that form the narrative of the player's playthrough.
    5|       |//! This system bridges the gap between mechanical simulation (ticks, resources) and
    6|       |//! player experience (stories, history).
    7|       |//!
    8|       |//! # Concepts
    9|       |//!
   10|       |//! * **`ChronicleEvent`**: An atomic piece of history (e.g., "Colony Founded", "First Winter").
   11|       |//! * **`EventImportance`**: Determines how prominent the event is in the UI.
   12|       |//! * **`Milestones`**: Automatic achievements tracked by the `check_milestones_system`.
   13|       |//!
   14|       |//! # Integration with Lore
   15|       |//!
   16|       |//! While the Chronicle stores *what* happened, the descriptions often come from the
   17|       |//! narrative generator (see `src/shared/narrative.rs`). This separation allows for
   18|       |//! flavor text to vary while the underlying event data remains consistent.
   19|       |
   20|       |use crate::layer1::balance::TICKS_PER_YEAR;
   21|       |use crate::layer1::building::{Building, BuildingType};
   22|       |use crate::shared::colony::ColonyName;
   23|       |use crate::shared::narrative::{NarrativeContext, NarrativeGenerator};
   24|       |use crate::shared::time::SimulationTime;
   25|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   26|       |
   27|       |/// Importance level for chronicle events.
   28|       |///
   29|       |/// This enum dictates visual hierarchy in the UI (colors, prefixes).
   30|       |///
   31|       |/// # Examples
   32|       |///
   33|       |/// ```
   34|       |/// use scale::layer1::chronicle::EventImportance;
   35|       |///
   36|       |/// let level = EventImportance::Legendary;
   37|       |/// assert!(matches!(level, EventImportance::Legendary));
   38|       |/// ```
   39|       |#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
   40|       |pub enum EventImportance {
   41|       |    /// Flavor text or minor notifications (e.g., "Bob ate a berry").
   42|       |    Minor,
   43|       |    /// Standard gameplay events (e.g., "Housing completed").
   44|       |    Standard,
   45|       |    /// Significant achievements or milestones (e.g., "Iron Age reached").
   46|       |    Major,
   47|       |    /// World-altering events or game start (e.g., "Colony Founded").
   48|       |    Legendary,
   49|       |}
   50|       |
   51|       |/// A single chronicle event.
   52|       |#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
   53|       |pub struct ChronicleEvent {
   54|       |    /// The simulation tick when the event occurred.
   55|       |    pub tick: u64,
   56|       |    /// The year when the event occurred (derived from tick).
   57|       |    pub year: u32,
   58|       |    /// The event description.
   59|       |    pub text: String,
   60|       |    /// The importance level of the event.
   61|       |    pub importance: EventImportance,
   62|       |}
   63|       |
   64|       |/// Chronicle resource - stores the colony's historical record.
   65|       |#[derive(Resource, Default)]
   66|       |pub struct Chronicle {
   67|       |    /// List of events in the chronicle.
   68|       |    pub events: Vec<ChronicleEvent>,
   69|       |}
   70|       |
   71|       |impl Chronicle {
   72|       |    /// Add a new event to the chronicle.
   73|       |    ///
   74|       |    /// The event is automatically timestamped with the current "Year" based on the tick.
   75|       |    ///
   76|       |    /// # Examples
   77|       |    ///
   78|       |    /// ```
   79|       |    /// use scale::layer1::chronicle::{Chronicle, EventImportance};
   80|       |    ///
   81|       |    /// let mut chronicle = Chronicle::default();
   82|       |    /// chronicle.add_event(100, "We built a fire.".to_string(), EventImportance::Standard);
   83|       |    /// assert_eq!(chronicle.events.len(), 1);
   84|       |    /// ```
   85|     26|    pub fn add_event(&mut self, tick: u64, text: String, importance: EventImportance) {
   86|     26|        self.events.push(ChronicleEvent {
   87|     26|            tick,
   88|     26|            year: 1 + u32::try_from(tick / TICKS_PER_YEAR).unwrap_or(u32::MAX), // Rough "year" approximation
   89|     26|            text,
   90|     26|            importance,
   91|     26|        });
   92|     26|    }
   93|       |
   94|       |    /// Add a pre-history event (year 0, tick 0) to the chronicle.
   95|       |    ///
   96|       |    /// These represent events that occurred before the colony was founded,

    1|       |//! Clothing and Temperature system.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! Handles Hypothermia in Winter and Clothing degradation.
    4|       |
    5|       |use crate::layer1::health::Health;
    6|       |use crate::layer1::pop::Pop;
    7|       |use crate::layer1::resources::ColonyResources;
    8|       |use crate::layer1::seasons::{Season, SeasonState};
    9|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   10|       |use rand::Rng;
   11|       |
   12|       |/// Applies hypothermia damage to pops without clothing during Winter.
   13|     14|pub fn hypothermia_system(
   14|     14|    mut pop_query: Query<&mut Health, With<Pop>>,
   15|     14|    resources: Res<ColonyResources>,
   16|     14|    season: Option<Res<SeasonState>>,
   17|     14|) {
   18|       |    // Only applies in Winter
   19|     14|    if !matches!(season.map(|s| s.current_season), Some(Season::Winter)) {
                      ^12
   20|     12|        return;
   21|      2|    }
   22|       |
   23|      2|    let clothing_available = resources.clothing;
   24|       |    #[allow(clippy::cast_precision_loss)]
   25|      2|    let pop_count = pop_query.iter().count() as f32;
   26|       |
   27|      2|    if pop_count == 0.0 {
   28|      0|        return;
   29|      2|    }
   30|       |
   31|       |    // Calculate shortage ratio (0.0 = full clothes, 1.0 = no clothes)
   32|       |    // If clothing >= pop_count, shortage = 0.
   33|       |    // If clothing = 0, shortage = 1.
   34|       |    // If clothing = pop_count / 2, shortage = 0.5.
   35|      2|    let shortage = (1.0 - (clothing_available / pop_count)).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
   36|       |
   37|      2|    if shortage <= 0.0 {
   38|      1|        return;
   39|      1|    }
   40|       |
   41|      1|    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
   42|       |
   43|      2|    for mut health in &mut pop_query {
                      ^1
   44|       |        // Probabilistic damage based on shortage
   45|      1|        if rng.gen_bool(f64::from(shortage)) {
   46|      1|            // Deal damage!
   47|      1|            health.take_damage(1.0);
   48|      1|        }
                      ^0
   49|       |    }
   50|     14|}
   51|       |
   52|       |/// Degrades clothing over time based on usage.
   53|     13|pub fn clothing_wear_system(pop_query: Query<&Pop>, mut resources: ResMut<ColonyResources>) {
   54|       |    #[allow(clippy::cast_precision_loss)]
   55|     13|    let pop_count = pop_query.iter().count() as f32;
   56|     13|    let used_clothing = resources.clothing.min(pop_count);
   57|       |
   58|     13|    if used_clothing <= 0.0 {
   59|     12|        return;
   60|      1|    }
   61|       |
   62|       |    // Simple decay: Fixed % chance per tick per used item
   63|       |    // OR simplified: decay = used_clothing * RATE
   64|      1|    let decay_rate = 0.001;
   65|      1|    let amount_lost = used_clothing * decay_rate;
   66|       |
   67|      1|    if amount_lost > 0.0 {
   68|      1|        // Direct subtraction, clamp to 0
   69|      1|        resources.clothing = (resources.clothing - amount_lost).max(0.0);
   70|      1|    }
                  ^0
   71|     13|}

    1|       |//! User intent and map designations.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! Designations are the primary way the player interacts with the simulation (The Mandate).
    4|       |//! Instead of directly manipulating entities, the player "designates" a tile for an action
    5|       |//! (e.g., "Mine here"), and the Pop AI (Souls) fulfills that request asynchronously.
    6|       |//!
    7|       |//! # Key Concepts
    8|       |//!
    9|       |//! * **Designation**: A persistent request attached to a specific map coordinate.
   10|       |//! * **DesignationType**: The kind of request (Mine, Demolish).
   11|       |//! * **Validation**: Rules for where designations can be placed (`can_designate`).
   12|       |
   13|       |use crate::layer1::{GridPosition, OccupiedTiles, TerrainGrid, TerrainType};
   14|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   15|       |
   16|       |/// Types of designations a player can apply to the map.
   17|       |#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug, Default)]
   18|       |pub enum DesignationType {
   19|       |    /// Designate a rock tile for mining.
   20|       |    #[default]
   21|       |    Mine,
   22|       |    /// Designate a building for demolition.
   23|       |    Demolish,
   24|       |    /// Designate a tree for chopping.
   25|       |    Chop,
   26|       |    /// Designate a building for repair.
   27|       |    Repair,
   28|       |}

  125|       |/// tiles[0] = TerrainType::Rock; // (0,0) is Rock

  127|       |/// world.insert_resource(OccupiedTiles::default());

  136|     29|pub fn can_designate(world: &World, x: i32, y: i32, designation_type: DesignationType) -> bool {
  137|       |    // Check bounds (basic check, more detailed check in terrain/occupied logic)
  138|     29|    if x < 0 || y < 0 {
  139|      0|        return false;
  140|     29|    }
  141|       |
  142|       |    // Check for existing designation at this location
  143|       |    // Note: This linear query might be slow for many designations,
  144|       |    // but acceptable for the MVP scope (designations < 1000).
  145|       |    // Future optimization: Spatial index for designations.
  146|     29|    let existing = world
  147|     29|        .iter_entities()
  148|     29|        .filter_map(|e| {
                                      ^19
  149|     19|            e.get::<GridPosition>()
  150|     19|                .and_then(|p| e.get::<Designation>().map(|_| p))
  151|     19|        })
  152|     29|        .any(|pos| pos.x == x && pos.y == y);
                                 ^19           ^4
  153|       |
  154|     29|    if existing {
  155|      1|        return false;
  156|     28|    }
  157|       |
  158|     28|    match designation_type {
  159|       |        DesignationType::Mine => {
  160|     24|            let terrain = world.resource::<TerrainGrid>();
  161|       |            // Allow casting because we checked for negative above
  162|     24|            terrain.get(x as usize, y as usize) == Some(TerrainType::Rock)
  163|       |        }
  164|       |        DesignationType::Demolish => {
  165|      2|            let occupied = world.resource::<OccupiedTiles>();
  166|       |            // Only occupied tiles can be demolished
  167|      2|            occupied.0.contains(&(x, y))
  168|       |        }
  169|       |        DesignationType::Chop => {
  170|      2|            let terrain = world.resource::<TerrainGrid>();
  171|       |            // Allow casting because we checked for negative above

  175|      0|            let occupied = world.resource::<OccupiedTiles>();
  176|       |            // Only occupied tiles can be repaired (assumes building)
  177|       |            // Ideally check if building has Structure and < Max HP, but for MVP check occupancy is enough
  178|      0|            occupied.0.contains(&(x, y))
  179|       |        }
  180|       |    }

  210|     23|pub fn try_designate(world: &mut World, x: i32, y: i32, designation_type: DesignationType) -> bool {
  211|     23|    if !can_designate(world, x, y, designation_type) {
  212|     11|        return false;
  213|     12|    }
  214|       |

  220|       |/// Designate all eligible tiles in a rectangle. Returns count of successful designations.
  221|       |///
  222|       |/// The rectangle is defined by two corners `(x1, y1)` and `(x2, y2)`. Corners can be
  223|       |/// given in any order; the function normalizes to min/max internally.
  224|       |///
  225|       |/// # Examples
  226|       |///
  227|       |/// ```
  228|       |/// use scale::layer1::designation::{try_designate_area, DesignationType};
  229|       |/// use scale::layer1::terrain::{TerrainGrid, TerrainType};
  230|       |/// use scale::layer1::building::OccupiedTiles;
  231|       |/// use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
  232|       |///
  233|       |/// let mut world = World::new();
  234|       |/// let mut tiles = vec![TerrainType::Grass; 100];
  235|       |/// tiles[55] = TerrainType::Rock; // (5,5)
  236|       |/// tiles[56] = TerrainType::Rock; // (6,5)
  237|       |/// world.insert_resource(TerrainGrid { width: 10, height: 10, tiles });
  238|       |/// world.insert_resource(OccupiedTiles::default());
  239|       |///
  240|       |/// assert_eq!(try_designate_area(&mut world, 5, 5, 6, 5, DesignationType::Mine), 2);
  241|       |/// ```
  242|      4|pub fn try_designate_area(
  243|      4|    world: &mut World,
  244|      4|    x1: i32,
  245|      4|    y1: i32,
  246|      4|    x2: i32,
  247|      4|    y2: i32,
  248|      4|    tool: DesignationType,
  249|      4|) -> u32 {
  250|      4|    let (min_x, max_x) = (x1.min(x2), x1.max(x2));
  251|      4|    let (min_y, max_y) = (y1.min(y2), y1.max(y2));
  252|      4|    let mut count = 0;
  253|      7|    for y in min_y..=max_y {
                           ^4      ^4
  254|     19|        for x in min_x..=max_x {
                               ^7      ^7
  255|     19|            if try_designate(world, x, y, tool) {
  256|      9|                count += 1;
  257|     10|            }
  258|       |        }
  259|       |    }
  260|      4|    count
  261|      4|}

    1|       |//! Execution layer bridging utility AI decisions to actual pop actions.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! This module handles:
    4|       |//! - Movement toward targets
    5|       |//! - Assigning pops to farms/housing
    6|       |//! - Executing work at designations (mining, chopping)
    7|       |//!
    8|       |//! # System Flow
    9|       |//!
   10|       |//! ```text
   11|       |//! evaluate_actions_system (existing)
   12|       |//!     ↓ inserts StartPlan
   13|       |//! cleanup_previous_assignment_system
   14|       |//!     ↓ removes pop from old farm/housing
   15|       |//! process_start_plan_system
   16|       |//!     ↓ converts StartPlan → MovementTarget
   17|       |//! movement_system
   18|       |//!     ↓ moves pop 1 tile, inserts AtTarget on arrival
   19|       |//! arrival_handler_system
   20|       |//!     ↓ assigns to farm/housing
   21|       |//! work_execution_system
   22|       |//!     ↓ calls mine_rock/chop_tree
   23|       |//! ```

  151|     28|    terrain: Res<TerrainGrid>,

  164|      4|                    (current_pos.x - target_pos.x).abs() + (current_pos.y - target_pos.y).abs();

  166|      0|                    commands.entity(pop_entity).insert(AtTarget);
  167|      0|                    continue;
  168|      4|                }
  169|     10|            }
  170|      2|        }

  173|      0|            continue;
  174|       |        };

  177|      2|            continue;

  191|      4|                let distance = (new_pos.x - target_pos.x).abs() + (new_pos.y - target_pos.y).abs();

  266|     42|fn is_walkable_terrain(terrain: &TerrainGrid, x: i32, y: i32) -> bool {

  268|     42|        terrain
  269|     42|            .get(x_idx, y_idx)

  277|     16|fn calculate_next_position(current: GridPosition, target: GridPosition) -> Option<GridPosition> {
  278|       |    // Calculate movement direction (Manhattan)
  279|     16|    let dx = (target.x - current.x).signum();
  280|     16|    let dy = (target.y - current.y).signum();
  281|       |
  282|       |    // Prefer horizontal movement, then vertical
  283|     16|    if dx != 0 {
  284|     15|        Some(GridPosition {
  285|     15|            x: current.x + dx,
  286|     15|            y: current.y,
  287|     15|        })
  288|       |    } else {
  289|      1|        Some(GridPosition {
  290|      1|            x: current.x,
  291|      1|            y: current.y + dy,
  292|      1|        })
  293|       |    }

  296|      1|fn execute_demolish(world: &mut World, designation_entity: Entity) -> bool {
  297|       |    // Find designation position
  298|      1|    world
  299|      1|        .get::<GridPosition>(designation_entity)
  300|      1|        .copied()
  301|      1|        .is_some_and(|designation_pos| {
  302|       |            // Find building at this position
  303|       |            // We collect to avoid borrow issues if we need to mutate world later
  304|      1|            let building_entity = world
  305|      1|                .query::<(Entity, &GridPosition, &Building)>()
  306|      1|                .iter(world)
  307|      1|                .find(|(_, pos, _)| pos.x == designation_pos.x && pos.y == designation_pos.y)
  308|      1|                .map(|(e, _, _)| e);
  309|       |
  310|      1|            if let Some(entity) = building_entity {
  311|      1|                world.despawn(entity);
  312|       |                // Remove from OccupiedTiles
  313|      1|                if let Some(mut occupied) = world.get_resource_mut::<OccupiedTiles>() {
  314|      1|                    occupied.0.remove(&(designation_pos.x, designation_pos.y));
  315|      1|                }
                              ^0
  316|      0|            }
  317|       |
  318|       |            // Despawn the designation itself
  319|      1|            world.despawn(designation_entity);
  320|      1|            true
  321|      1|        })

  324|       |/// Executes work at designations when pop is at target with Work action.
  325|     62|pub fn work_execution_system(world: &mut World) {
  326|       |    // Check tools at the start of the system
  327|     62|    let (has_tools, mut tool_broken) = {
  328|     62|        let res = world.resource::<ColonyResources>();
  329|     62|        (res.tools >= 1.0, false)
  330|     62|    };
  331|       |
  332|     62|    let tool_efficiency = if has_tools { 1.0 } else { NO_TOOL_PENALTY };
                                                                    ^0
  333|       |
  334|       |    // Find pops at their work target and capture their morale
  335|       |    // Since we need to access Needs which is a component, and we need &mut World later,
  336|       |    // we should collect Needs data first.
  337|     62|    let workers_data: Vec<(Entity, Entity, f32, ActionType)> = world
  338|     62|        .query_filtered::<(Entity, &MovementTarget, Option<&Needs>, Option<&Memories>), With<AtTarget>>()
  339|     62|        .iter(world)
  340|     62|        .filter(|(_, mt, _, _)| mt.for_action == ActionType::Work || mt.for_action == ActionType::Repair)
                                              ^47                                  ^0
  341|     62|        .map(|(e, mt, needs, memories)| {
                                                      ^47
  342|     47|            let morale = needs.map_or(0.5, |n| {
                                                             ^41
  343|     41|                memories.map_or_else(
  344|     41|                    || n.morale(),
  345|      0|                    |m| calculate_effective_morale(n, m),
  346|       |                )
  347|     41|            });
  348|     47|            (e, mt.target_entity, morale, mt.for_action)
  349|     47|        })
  350|     62|        .collect();
  351|       |
  352|    109|    for (pop_entity, designation_entity, morale, action_type) in workers_data {
                       ^47         ^47                 ^47     ^47
  353|     47|        let morale_efficiency = get_morale_efficiency(morale);
  354|     47|        let work_amount = WORK_PER_TICK * tool_efficiency * morale_efficiency;
  355|       |
  356|       |        // Check if designation still exists
  357|     47|        if world.get_entity(designation_entity).is_err() {
  358|      1|            cleanup_pop_work_state(world, pop_entity);
  359|      1|            continue;
  360|     46|        }
  361|       |
  362|       |        // Get designation type
  363|     46|        let Some(designation) = world.get::<Designation>(designation_entity) else {
  364|      0|            continue;
  365|       |        };
  366|     46|        let designation_type = designation.designation_type;
  367|       |
  368|     46|        let worked = match designation_type {
  369|       |            DesignationType::Mine => {
  370|     44|                process_mining(world, designation_entity, work_amount);
  371|     44|                true
  372|       |            }
  373|       |            DesignationType::Chop => {
  374|      1|                process_logging(world, designation_entity, work_amount);
  375|      1|                true
  376|       |            }
  377|      1|            DesignationType::Demolish => execute_demolish(world, designation_entity),
  378|       |            DesignationType::Repair => {
  379|      0|                crate::layer1::structure::process_repair(world, designation_entity, work_amount);
  380|      0|                true
  381|       |            }
  382|       |        };
  383|       |
  384|       |        // After work: if designation was despawned (work completed), reset pop state
  385|     46|        if world.get_entity(designation_entity).is_err() {
  386|      3|            cleanup_pop_work_state(world, pop_entity);
  387|     43|        }
  388|       |
  389|       |        // Workplace Hazards
  390|     46|        if worked {
  391|     46|            handle_workplace_hazards(world, pop_entity, action_type);
  392|       |
  393|     46|            if has_tools && !tool_broken {
  394|     46|                let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
  395|     46|                if rng.gen_bool(TOOL_BREAK_CHANCE) {
  396|      1|                    tool_broken = true;
  397|     45|                }
  398|      0|            }
  399|      0|        }
  400|       |    }
  401|       |
  402|     62|    if tool_broken {
  403|      1|        let mut res = world.resource_mut::<ColonyResources>();
  404|      1|        if res.tools >= 1.0 {
  405|      1|            res.tools -= 1.0;
  406|      1|        }
                      ^0
  407|     61|    }
  408|     62|}

  413|      4|        .remove::<MovementTarget>()
  414|      4|        .remove::<AtTarget>();

                              ^3
  416|      3|        action.current = ActionType::Idle;

  446|     46|    let danger = action_type.danger_level();

  456|      1|                log.add(format!("ACCIDENT: Worker injured! (-{damage} HP)"));
  457|      1|            }
                          ^0
  458|      0|        }
  459|     45|    }

 1075|       |        );

 1080|      1|        assert!(
 1081|      1|            (action.current_utility - 0.0).abs() < f32::EPSILON,

 1137|       |    #[test]
 1138|      1|    fn test_cleanup_removes_from_farm() {
 1139|      1|        let mut world = setup_world();
 1140|       |
 1141|      1|        let pop = world.spawn(Pop).id();
 1142|      1|        let farm = world
 1143|      1|            .spawn((
 1144|      1|                Building {
 1145|      1|                    building_type: BuildingType::Farm,
 1146|      1|                },
 1147|      1|                GridPosition { x: 5, y: 5 },
 1148|      1|                Farm {
 1149|      1|                    capacity: 2,
 1150|      1|                    workers: vec![pop],
 1151|      1|                },
 1152|      1|            ))
 1153|      1|            .id();
 1154|       |
 1155|       |        // Pop is assigned and wants to switch
 1156|      1|        world.entity_mut(pop).insert((
 1157|      1|            AssignedTo {
 1158|      1|                entity: farm,
 1159|      1|                assignment_type: AssignmentType::FarmWorker,
 1160|      1|            },
 1161|      1|            StartPlan {
 1162|      1|                action: ActionType::SatisfyRest,
 1163|      1|                target: None,
 1164|      1|            },
 1165|      1|        ));
 1166|       |
 1167|      1|        world
 1168|      1|            .run_system_once(cleanup_previous_assignment_system)
 1169|      1|            .unwrap();
 1170|       |
 1171|       |        // Pop should be removed from farm workers
 1172|      1|        let farm_comp = world.get::<Farm>(farm).unwrap();
 1173|      1|        assert!(!farm_comp.workers.contains(&pop));
 1174|       |
 1175|       |        // AssignedTo should be removed
 1176|      1|        assert!(world.get::<AssignedTo>(pop).is_none());
 1177|      1|    }

 1253|      1|    fn test_full_execution_flow_farm() {
 1254|      1|        let mut world = setup_world();
 1255|       |
 1256|      1|        let farm = world
 1257|      1|            .spawn((
 1258|      1|                Building {
 1259|      1|                    building_type: BuildingType::Farm,
 1260|      1|                },
 1261|      1|                GridPosition { x: 2, y: 0 },
 1262|      1|                Farm::default(),
 1263|      1|            ))
 1264|      1|            .id();
 1265|       |
 1266|      1|        let pop = world
 1267|      1|            .spawn((
 1268|      1|                Pop,
 1269|      1|                GridPosition { x: 0, y: 0 },
 1270|      1|                Needs::default(),
 1271|      1|                PopAction::default(),
 1272|      1|                UtilityWeights::default(),
 1273|      1|                StartPlan {
 1274|      1|                    action: ActionType::SatisfyHunger,

 1277|      1|            ))
 1278|      1|            .id();

 1338|       |    #[test]
 1339|      1|    fn test_movement_persists_across_evaluation_cycles() {
 1340|       |        use crate::layer1::utility_ai::{
 1341|       |            UtilityConfig, evaluate_actions_system, update_action_timer_system,
 1342|       |        };
 1343|       |        use crate::shared::time::SimulationTime;
 1344|       |
 1345|      1|        let mut world = setup_world();
 1346|      1|        world.insert_resource(UtilityConfig::default());
 1347|      1|        world.insert_resource(SimulationTime::default());
 1348|       |
 1349|       |        // Put a rock at (5, 0) for mining
 1350|      1|        {
 1351|      1|            let mut terrain = world.resource_mut::<TerrainGrid>();
 1352|      1|            terrain.tiles[5] = TerrainType::Rock;
 1353|      1|        }
 1354|       |
 1355|       |        // Create a mining designation
 1356|      1|        let _designation = world
 1357|      1|            .spawn((
 1358|      1|                Designation {
 1359|      1|                    designation_type: DesignationType::Mine,
 1360|      1|                },
 1361|      1|                GridPosition { x: 5, y: 0 },
 1362|      1|            ))
 1363|      1|            .id();
 1364|       |
 1365|       |        // Create a pop that will want to work
 1366|      1|        let pop = world
 1367|      1|            .spawn((
 1368|      1|                Pop,
 1369|      1|                GridPosition { x: 0, y: 0 },
 1370|      1|                Needs::default(),
 1371|      1|                PopAction::default(),
 1372|      1|                UtilityWeights::default(),
 1373|      1|            ))
 1374|      1|            .id();
 1375|       |
 1376|       |        // Tick 1: Pop should decide to work
 1377|      1|        world.run_system_once(update_action_timer_system).unwrap();
 1378|      1|        evaluate_actions_system(&mut world);
 1379|       |
 1380|       |        // Should have StartPlan for work
 1381|      1|        assert!(
 1382|      1|            world.get::<StartPlan>(pop).is_some(),
 1383|      0|            "Pop should have StartPlan after first evaluation"
 1384|       |        );
 1385|       |
 1386|       |        // Process and start moving
 1387|      1|        world
 1388|      1|            .run_system_once(cleanup_previous_assignment_system)
 1389|      1|            .unwrap();
 1390|      1|        world.run_system_once(process_start_plan_system).unwrap();
 1391|      1|        world.run_system_once(movement_system).unwrap();
 1392|      1|        world.run_system_once(arrival_handler_system).unwrap();
 1393|      1|        work_execution_system(&mut world);
 1394|       |
 1395|      1|        let pos1 = world.get::<GridPosition>(pop).unwrap();
 1396|      1|        assert_eq!(pos1.x, 1, "Pop should have moved to x=1");
                                            ^0
 1397|       |
 1398|       |        // Tick 2-4: Continue moving toward rock (stop adjacent at x=4)
 1399|       |        // Pop can't stand ON the rock, so they work from adjacent tile
 1400|      4|        for tick in 2..=4 {
                          ^3
 1401|      3|            world.run_system_once(update_action_timer_system).unwrap();
 1402|      3|            evaluate_actions_system(&mut world);
 1403|      3|            world
 1404|      3|                .run_system_once(cleanup_previous_assignment_system)
 1405|      3|                .unwrap();
 1406|      3|            world.run_system_once(process_start_plan_system).unwrap();
 1407|      3|            world.run_system_once(movement_system).unwrap();
 1408|      3|            world.run_system_once(arrival_handler_system).unwrap();
 1409|      3|            work_execution_system(&mut world);
 1410|       |
 1411|      3|            let pos = *world.get::<GridPosition>(pop).unwrap();
 1412|      3|            assert_eq!(pos.x, tick, "Tick {tick}: Pop should be at x={tick}");
                                                  ^0
 1413|       |        }
 1414|       |
 1415|       |        // After tick 4, pop should be adjacent to rock (at x=4) and marked AtTarget
 1416|      1|        assert!(

   28|       |/// Produces food from all farms with workers.
   29|    617|pub fn produce_food_system(
   30|    617|    farm_query: Query<(&Farm, &crate::layer1::building::Building)>,
   31|    617|    pop_query: Query<&Pop>,
   32|    617|    season: Option<Res<SeasonState>>,
   33|    617|    mut resources: ResMut<ColonyResources>,
   34|    617|) {
   35|    617|    let modifier = season.map_or(1.0, |s| s.current_season.food_modifier());
                                                        ^14              ^14
   36|       |
   37|  1.22k|    for (farm, building) in &farm_query {
                       ^605  ^605
   38|       |        #[allow(clippy::cast_precision_loss)]
   39|    605|        let worker_count = farm
   40|    605|            .workers
   41|    605|            .iter()
   42|    606|            .filter(|&&e| pop_query.get(e).is_ok())
                           ^605
   43|    605|            .count() as f32;
   44|       |

   46|       |

   55|    604|                }
   56|       |            }
   57|      0|        }
   58|       |    }

   61|       |/// Pops eat food when hungry.
   62|     16|pub fn consume_food_system(
   63|     16|    mut pop_query: Query<(Entity, &mut Needs), With<Pop>>,
   64|     16|    mut resources: ResMut<ColonyResources>,
   65|     16|) {
   66|     16|    if resources.food < f32::EPSILON {
   67|      0|        return;
   68|     16|    }
   69|       |
   70|     16|    let mut food = resources.food;
   71|       |
   72|       |    // Collect hungry pop entities first to avoid borrow issues with mut iteration
   73|     16|    let hungry_pops: Vec<Entity> = pop_query
   74|     16|        .iter()
   75|     65|        .filter(|(_, needs)| needs.hunger < FOOD_HUNGER_THRESHOLD)
                       ^16
   76|     16|        .map(|(e, _)| e)
   77|     16|        .collect();
   78|       |
   79|     18|    for entity in hungry_pops {
                      ^3
   80|      3|        if food < FOOD_PER_MEAL {
   81|      1|            break;
   82|      2|        }
   83|       |
   84|      2|        if let Ok((_, mut needs)) = pop_query.get_mut(entity) {
   85|      2|            food -= FOOD_PER_MEAL;
   86|      2|            needs.hunger = (needs.hunger + HUNGER_PER_MEAL).min(1.0);
   87|      2|        }
                      ^0
   88|       |    }
   89|       |
   90|     16|    resources.food = food;
   91|     16|}

   53|    122|    let mut new_fires = Vec::new();
   54|    122|    let mut fire_locations = HashSet::new();
   55|       |
   56|       |    // 1. Collect all current fire locations to avoid re-igniting or double-checking
   57|    122|    let mut fire_query = world.query::<(&GridPosition, &Fire)>();

                                  ^122       ^122 ^122
   59|    198|        fire_locations.insert(*pos);

   61|       |

   63|       |    // We need to access terrain, so we scope the immutable borrow here
   64|    122|    let (width, height, tiles) = {

   66|    122|        (terrain.width, terrain.height, terrain.tiles.clone())
   67|    122|    };

   72|       |    // Query must be read-only here if we are pushing to a vec.

   92|      0|                continue;
   93|    616|            }

   96|       |

  102|       |                    // Tree flammability chance
  103|      6|                    if rng.gen_bool(0.1) {
  104|      1|                        // 10% chance per tick per neighbor
  105|      1|                        should_ignite = true;
  106|      5|                    }
  107|    610|                }
  108|      0|            }
  109|       |

  138|       |/// System that handles fire damage and burning out.
  139|     13|pub fn fire_damage_system(world: &mut World) {
  140|       |    // Decrement lifetime, destroy burnt objects
  141|     13|    let mut fires_to_remove = Vec::new();
  142|     13|    let mut terrain_changes = Vec::new(); // (x, y, NewType)
  143|     13|    let mut burnt_entities = Vec::new(); // Entities at position to check for destruction
  144|       |
  145|     13|    let mut query = world.query::<(Entity, &GridPosition, &mut Fire)>();
  146|     13|    for (entity, pos, mut fire) in query.iter_mut(world) {
                       ^1      ^1   ^1
  147|      1|        if fire.lifetime > 0 {
  148|      1|            fire.lifetime -= 1;
  149|      1|        }
                      ^0
  150|       |
  151|      1|        if fire.lifetime == 0 {
  152|      1|            fires_to_remove.push((entity, *pos));
  153|      1|            burnt_entities.push(*pos);
  154|      1|        }
                      ^0
  155|       |    }
  156|       |
  157|       |    // Apply destruction for burnt-out fires
  158|     13|    let mut entities_to_despawn = Vec::new();
  159|       |    {
  160|     13|        let terrain = world.resource::<TerrainGrid>();
  161|       |
  162|       |        // Process removals and terrain/building destruction
  163|     14|        for (entity, pos) in &fires_to_remove {
                           ^1      ^1
  164|      1|            entities_to_despawn.push(*entity);
  165|       |
  166|       |            // If it was on a tree, turn to dirt
  167|      1|            if let Some(tile) = terrain.get(pos.x as usize, pos.y as usize) {
  168|      1|                if tile == TerrainType::Tree {
  169|      1|                    terrain_changes.push((pos.x, pos.y, TerrainType::Dirt));
  170|      1|                }
                              ^0
  171|      0|            }
  172|       |        }
  173|       |    }
  174|       |
  175|     14|    for entity in entities_to_despawn {
                      ^1
  176|      1|        world.despawn(entity);
  177|      1|    }
  178|       |
  179|       |    // Apply terrain changes
  180|     13|    if !terrain_changes.is_empty() {
  181|      1|        let mut terrain_mut = world.resource_mut::<TerrainGrid>();
  182|      2|        for (x, y, new_type) in terrain_changes {
                           ^1 ^1 ^1
  183|      1|            let idx = (y as usize) * terrain_mut.width + (x as usize);
  184|      1|            if idx < terrain_mut.tiles.len() {
  185|      1|                terrain_mut.tiles[idx] = new_type;
  186|      1|            }
                          ^0
  187|       |        }
  188|     12|    }
  189|       |
  190|       |    // Destroy flammable entities at burnt locations
  191|       |    // We do this by finding all Flammable entities at the burnt positions
  192|       |    // This requires a query scan which is O(N_buildings * N_burnt_tiles), ok for MVP
  193|     13|    if !burnt_entities.is_empty() {
  194|      1|        let mut entities_to_destroy = Vec::new();
  195|       |        // Check for Structure component to avoid destroying durable buildings
  196|      1|        let mut query = world.query::<(Entity, &GridPosition, &Flammable, Option<&Structure>)>();
  197|       |
  198|      1|        for (entity, pos, _flammable, structure) in query.iter(world) {
                           ^0      ^0   ^0          ^0
  199|      0|            if burnt_entities.contains(pos) {
  200|       |                // If it's a structure, it survives the fire burning out (unless HP was 0, handled elsewhere)
  201|      0|                if structure.is_some() {
  202|      0|                    continue;
  203|      0|                }
  204|      0|                entities_to_destroy.push(entity);
  205|      0|            }
  206|       |        }
  207|       |
  208|      1|        for entity in entities_to_destroy {
                          ^0
  209|      0|            world.despawn(entity);
  210|      0|        }
  211|     12|    }

   62|       |/// Despawns entities that have lost all health.
   63|     13|pub fn death_system(world: &mut World) {
   64|     13|    let tick = world
   65|     13|        .get_resource::<crate::shared::time::SimulationTime>()

   67|       |
   68|       |    // Collect entities to despawn (can't modify world during iteration)
   69|     13|    let to_despawn: Vec<Entity> = world

   72|     62|        .filter(|(_, h)| !h.is_alive())

   74|     13|        .collect();

                      ^0
   87|       |    }

   89|       |    // Add WitnessedDeath memory to all survivors
   90|      1|    let mut query = world.query::<&mut Memories>();
   91|      1|    for mut memories in query.iter_mut(world) {
                      ^0
   92|      0|        for _ in 0..death_count {
   93|      0|            memories.add(MemoryType::WitnessedDeath, tick);
   94|      0|        }
   95|       |    }

/app/src/layer1/housing.rs:
    1|       |//! Housing and population shelter management.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! Housing provides shelter for Pops, allowing them to recover from fatigue (Rest).
    4|       |//! Without housing, pops will sleep on the ground (recovering slower and taking penalties).
    5|       |//!
    6|       |//! # Key Concepts
    7|       |//!
    8|       |//! * **Housing**: A component on buildings that tracks capacity and residents.
    9|       |//! * **Assignment**: Pops are assigned to a specific housing entity.
   10|       |//! * **Restoration**: The `restore_rest_in_housing_system` ticks up the rest need of residents.
   11|       |
   12|       |use crate::layer1::needs::Needs;
   13|       |use crate::layer1::pop::Pop;
   14|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   15|       |
   16|       |/// Housing component - provides shelter and rest for pops.
   17|       |///
   18|       |/// Attached to buildings like Cabins or Dormitories.
   19|       |///
   20|       |/// # Examples
   21|       |///
   22|       |/// ```
   23|       |/// use scale::layer1::housing::Housing;
   24|       |///
   25|       |/// let housing = Housing {
   26|       |///     capacity: 5,
   27|       |///     residents: Vec::new(),
   28|       |/// };
   29|       |/// assert_eq!(housing.capacity, 5);
   30|       |/// ```
   31|       |#[derive(Component)]
   32|       |pub struct Housing {
   33|       |    /// Maximum number of residents this housing can hold.
   34|       |    pub capacity: usize,
   35|       |    /// List of residents currently assigned to this housing.
   36|       |    pub residents: Vec<Entity>,
   37|       |}
   38|       |
   39|       |impl Default for Housing {
   40|     17|    fn default() -> Self {
   41|     17|        Self {
   42|     17|            capacity: 2,
   43|     17|            residents: Vec::new(),
   44|     17|        }
   45|     17|    }
   46|       |}
   47|       |
   48|       |const REST_RESTORE_PER_TICK: f32 = 0.05; // Full rest in ~20 ticks
   49|       |
   50|       |/// Restores rest for all pops residing in housing.
   51|       |///
   52|       |/// This system should run every tick. It iterates over all housing entities
   53|       |/// and applies rest restoration to their assigned residents.
   54|       |///
   55|       |/// # Examples
   56|       |///
   57|       |/// ```
   58|       |/// use scale::layer1::housing::{Housing, restore_rest_in_housing_system};
   59|       |/// use scale::layer1::needs::Needs;
   60|       |/// use scale::layer1::pop::Pop;
   61|       |/// use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   62|       |/// use bevy_ecs::system::RunSystemOnce;
   63|       |///
   64|       |/// let mut world = World::new();
   65|       |///
   66|       |/// // Create a tired pop
   67|       |/// let pop = world.spawn((Pop, Needs { rest: 0.1, ..Default::default() })).id();
   68|       |///
   69|       |/// // Assign to housing
   70|       |/// world.spawn(Housing {
   71|       |///     capacity: 1,
   72|       |///     residents: vec![pop],
   73|       |/// });
   74|       |///
   75|       |/// // Run system
   76|       |/// world.run_system_once(restore_rest_in_housing_system).unwrap();
   77|       |///
   78|       |/// // Check result
   79|       |/// let needs = world.get::<Needs>(pop).unwrap();
   80|       |/// assert!(needs.rest > 0.1);
   81|       |/// ```
   82|     15|pub fn restore_rest_in_housing_system(
   83|     15|    housing_query: Query<&Housing>,
   84|     15|    mut needs_query: Query<&mut Needs>,
   85|     15|) {
   86|     18|    for housing in &housing_query {
                      ^3
   87|      7|        for &resident in &housing.residents {
                           ^4
   88|      4|            if let Ok(mut needs) = needs_query.get_mut(resident) {
   89|      4|                needs.rest = (needs.rest + REST_RESTORE_PER_TICK).min(1.0);
   90|      4|            }
                          ^0
   91|       |        }
   92|       |    }
   93|     15|}
   94|       |
   95|       |/// Removes dead residents from housing.
   96|       |///
   97|       |/// This creates a self-healing relationship between housing and pops.
   98|       |/// If a pop dies (despawns), this system ensures the housing slot is freed.
   99|       |///
  100|       |/// # Examples
  101|       |///
  102|       |/// ```
  103|       |/// use scale::layer1::housing::{Housing, clean_dead_residents_system};
  104|       |/// use scale::layer1::pop::Pop;
  105|       |/// use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
  106|       |/// use bevy_ecs::system::RunSystemOnce;
  107|       |///
  108|       |/// let mut world = World::new();
  109|       |/// let pop = world.spawn(Pop).id();
  110|       |/// let housing = world.spawn(Housing {
  111|       |///     capacity: 1,
  112|       |///     residents: vec![pop],
  113|       |/// }).id();
  114|       |///
  115|       |/// // Kill pop
  116|       |/// world.despawn(pop);
  117|       |///
  118|       |/// // Cleanup
  119|       |/// world.run_system_once(clean_dead_residents_system).unwrap();
  120|       |///
  121|       |/// // Verify
  122|       |/// let h = world.get::<Housing>(housing).unwrap();
  123|       |/// assert!(h.residents.is_empty());
  124|       |/// ```
  125|     14|pub fn clean_dead_residents_system(mut housing_query: Query<&mut Housing>, pop_query: Query<&Pop>) {
  126|     16|    for mut housing in &mut housing_query {
                      ^2
  127|      2|        housing
  128|      2|            .residents
  129|      3|            .retain(|&resident| pop_query.get(resident).is_ok());
                           ^2
  130|       |    }
  131|     14|}
