/app/src/layer1/integration.rs:
    1|       |//! Integration systems that bridge multiple domains in Layer 1.
    2|       |
    3|       |use crate::layer1::fire::Fire;
    4|       |use crate::layer1::health::Health;
    5|       |use crate::layer1::map::GridPosition;
    6|       |use crate::layer1::pop::Pop;
    7|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
    8|       |use std::collections::HashSet;
    9|       |
   10|       |/// Applies damage to pops standing on fire.
   11|       |///
   12|       |/// Bridges the Fire system (Environment) and Pop Health system (Simulation).
   13|     12|pub fn fire_damage_pops_system(
   14|     12|    fire_query: Query<(&GridPosition, &Fire)>,
   15|     12|    mut pop_query: Query<(&GridPosition, &mut Health), With<Pop>>,
   16|     12|) {
   17|       |    // 1. Identify dangerous tiles
   18|     12|    let fire_tiles: HashSet<GridPosition> = fire_query.iter().map(|(pos, _)| *pos).collect();
   19|       |
   20|     12|    if fire_tiles.is_empty() {
   21|     12|        return;
   22|      0|    }
   23|       |
   24|       |    // 2. Apply damage to pops on those tiles
   25|      0|    for (pos, mut health) in &mut pop_query {
   26|      0|        if fire_tiles.contains(pos) {
   27|      0|            // Apply 5.0 damage per tick (20 ticks to die)
   28|      0|            let damage = 5.0;
   29|      0|            health.take_damage(damage);
   30|      0|        }
   31|       |    }
   32|     12|}

   11|       |pub struct NamedLocations {
   12|       |    /// A map from grid coordinates (x, y) to location names.
   13|       |    pub names: HashMap<(i32, i32), String>,
   14|       |}
   15|       |

   17|       |    /// Name a specific coordinate. Overwrites existing names.

   19|     13|        self.names.insert((x, y), name);
   20|     13|    }

   22|       |    /// Get the name of a location, if it exists.

   28|       |
   29|       |/// Names the starting location based on the first pop found.
   30|      9|pub fn initial_naming_system(world: &mut World) {
   31|      9|    let mut query = world.query_filtered::<&GridPosition, With<Pop>>();
   32|      9|    let start_pos = query.iter(world).next().map(|pos| (pos.x, pos.y));
   33|       |
   34|      9|    if let Some((x, y)) = start_pos {
   35|      9|        let name = world
   36|      9|            .resource::<NarrativeGenerator>()
   37|      9|            .get_random_fragment("LANDING_NAME")
   38|      9|            .cloned()
   39|      9|            .unwrap_or_else(|| "Landing Site".to_string());
                                             ^0             ^0
   40|       |
   41|      9|        let mut locations = world.resource_mut::<NamedLocations>();
   42|      9|        locations.add(x, y, name.clone());
   43|       |
   44|      9|        let tick = world.resource::<SimulationTime>().tick;
   45|      9|        let mut chronicle = world.resource_mut::<Chronicle>();
   46|      9|        chronicle.add_event(
   47|      9|            tick,
   48|      9|            format!("We name this place {name}. Here we begin."),
   49|      9|            EventImportance::Standard,
   50|      9|        );
   51|      0|    }

/app/src/layer1/map.rs:
    1|       |//! Spatial primitives and map utilities.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! This module defines the foundational spatial component `GridPosition`.
    4|       |//!
    5|       |//! # The Coordinate System
    6|       |//!
    7|       |//! The world is represented as a 2D grid. The coordinate system follows these rules:
    8|       |//! * **Origin (0,0)**: The top-left corner of the map.
    9|       |//! * **X-Axis**: Increases to the right (East).
   10|       |//! * **Y-Axis**: Increases downwards (South).
   11|       |//!
   12|       |//! While `GridPosition` uses `i32` to allow for flexibility (and potentially negative
   13|       |//! coordinates for off-map entities or infinite scrolling in the future), the valid
   14|       |//! gameplay area is typically bounded by the `TerrainGrid` dimensions (0..width, 0..height).
   15|       |//!
   16|       |//! # Relationship with `TerrainGrid`
   17|       |//!
   18|       |//! The `TerrainGrid` resource stores tile data in a flat vector. To access tile data
   19|       |//! for a given `GridPosition`, you must convert the (x, y) coordinates to a linear index:
   20|       |//! `index = y * width + x`.
   21|       |//!
   22|       |//! Always check bounds before accessing the grid, as `GridPosition` does not enforce
   23|       |//! map limits itself.
   24|       |
   25|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   26|       |
   27|       |/// Grid position in world space.
   28|       |///
   29|       |/// This component marks an entity's physical location in the colony. It is used by
   30|       |/// rendering systems, pathfinding, and spatial queries.
   31|       |///
   32|       |/// # Examples
   33|       |///
   34|       |/// Basic usage:
   35|       |/// ```
   36|       |/// use scale::layer1::map::GridPosition;
   37|       |///
   38|       |/// let pos = GridPosition { x: 10, y: 5 };
   39|       |/// assert_eq!(pos.x, 10);
   40|       |/// ```
   41|       |///
   42|       |/// Calculating Manhattan distance:
   43|       |/// ```
   44|       |/// use scale::layer1::map::GridPosition;
   45|       |///
   46|       |/// let a = GridPosition { x: 0, y: 0 };
   47|       |/// let b = GridPosition { x: 3, y: 4 };
   48|       |/// let distance = (a.x - b.x).abs() + (a.y - b.y).abs();
   49|       |/// assert_eq!(distance, 7);
   50|       |/// ```
   51|       |#[derive(Component, Default, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
   52|       |pub struct GridPosition {
   53|       |    /// The X coordinate (horizontal column).
   54|       |    pub x: i32,
   55|       |    /// The Y coordinate (vertical row).
   56|       |    pub y: i32,
   57|       |}
   58|       |
   59|       |#[cfg(test)]
   60|       |mod tests {

    1|       |use crate::layer1::needs::Needs;

    3|       |
    4|       |/// Types of memories a pop can acquire.
    5|       |#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
    6|       |pub enum MemoryType {
    7|       |    /// Witnessed a pop die.
    8|       |    WitnessedDeath,
    9|       |    /// Suffered from starvation (hunger = 0).
   10|       |    StarvationTrauma,
   11|       |    /// Ate a high quality meal.
   12|       |    AteFineMeal,
   13|       |    /// Won a combat encounter.
   14|       |    WonFight,
   15|       |}
   16|       |
   17|       |impl MemoryType {
   18|       |    /// Returns the mood impact (0.0 to 1.0 or negative).
   19|       |    #[must_use]
   20|      5|    pub const fn base_mood_impact(&self) -> f32 {
   21|      5|        match self {
   22|      3|            Self::WitnessedDeath => -0.2,
   23|      0|            Self::StarvationTrauma => -0.15,
   24|      2|            Self::AteFineMeal => 0.1,
   25|      0|            Self::WonFight => 0.05,
   26|       |        }
   27|      5|    }
   28|       |
   29|       |    /// Returns the decay rate per tick.
   30|       |    #[must_use]
   31|      2|    pub const fn decay_rate(&self) -> f32 {
   32|       |        // Ticks to fade completely
   33|      2|        match self {
   34|      2|            Self::WitnessedDeath => 0.0005,              // Slow fade (2000 ticks)
   35|      0|            Self::StarvationTrauma => 0.001,             // Medium
   36|      0|            Self::AteFineMeal | Self::WonFight => 0.002, // Fast (500 ticks)
   37|       |        }
   38|      2|    }
   39|       |}
   40|       |
   41|       |/// A specific instance of a memory on a pop.
   42|       |#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
   43|       |pub struct ActiveMemory {
   44|       |    /// The type of memory.
   45|       |    pub memory_type: MemoryType,
   46|       |    /// The tick when it was added.
   47|       |    pub added_at: u64,
   48|       |    /// Current intensity (starts at 1.0, decays to 0.0).
   49|       |    pub intensity: f32,
   50|       |}
   51|       |
   52|       |/// Component storing all active memories for a pop.
   53|       |#[derive(Component, Default, Debug, Clone)]
   54|       |pub struct Memories {
   55|       |    /// List of active memories.
   56|       |    pub items: Vec<ActiveMemory>,
   57|       |}
   58|       |
   59|       |impl Memories {
   60|       |    /// Adds a new memory with full intensity.
   61|      7|    pub fn add(&mut self, memory_type: MemoryType, current_tick: u64) {
   62|      7|        self.items.push(ActiveMemory {
   63|      7|            memory_type,
   64|      7|            added_at: current_tick,
   65|      7|            intensity: 1.0,
   66|      7|        });
   67|      7|    }
   68|       |
   69|       |    /// Decays all memories by the given number of ticks.
   70|       |    /// Removes memories that have faded to 0 intensity.
   71|     62|    pub fn decay(&mut self, ticks_passed: u64) {
   72|       |        #[allow(clippy::cast_precision_loss)]
   73|     62|        let ticks_f32 = ticks_passed as f32;
   74|     64|        for memory in &mut self.items {
                          ^2
   75|      2|            let decay = memory.memory_type.decay_rate() * ticks_f32;
   76|      2|            memory.intensity = (memory.intensity - decay).max(0.0);
   77|      2|        }
   78|     62|        self.items.retain(|m| m.intensity > 0.0);
                                            ^2
   79|     62|    }
   80|       |}
   81|       |
   82|       |/// Calculates the effective morale including memory modifiers.
   83|       |#[must_use]
   84|      4|pub fn calculate_effective_morale(needs: &Needs, memories: &Memories) -> f32 {
   85|      4|    let base = needs.morale();
   86|      4|    let memory_modifier: f32 = memories
   87|      4|        .items
   88|      4|        .iter()
   89|      5|        .map(|m| m.memory_type.base_mood_impact() * m.intensity)
                       ^4
   90|      4|        .sum();
   91|       |
   92|      4|    (base + memory_modifier).clamp(0.0, 1.0)
   93|      4|}
   94|       |
   95|       |/// System to decay memories every tick.
   96|     12|pub fn memory_decay_system(mut query: Query<&mut Memories>) {
   97|       |    // Assuming this runs every tick
   98|     60|    query.par_iter_mut().for_each(|mut memories| {
                  ^12   ^12            ^12
   99|     60|        memories.decay(1);
  100|     60|    });
  101|     12|}

/app/src/layer1/needs.rs:
    1|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
    2|       |
    3|       |/// Pop survival needs.
    4|       |#[derive(Component, Clone, Copy, Debug)]
    5|       |pub struct Needs {
    6|       |    /// Hunger level: 0.0 = starving, 1.0 = full.
    7|       |    pub hunger: f32,
    8|       |    /// Rest level: 0.0 = exhausted, 1.0 = rested.
    9|       |    pub rest: f32,
   10|       |    /// Leisure level: 0.0 = bored, 1.0 = entertained.
   11|       |    pub leisure: f32,
   12|       |}
   13|       |
   14|       |impl Default for Needs {
   15|    113|    fn default() -> Self {
   16|    113|        Self {
   17|    113|            hunger: 0.8,
   18|    113|            rest: 0.8,
   19|    113|            leisure: 0.8,
   20|    113|        }
   21|    113|    }
   22|       |}
   23|       |
   24|       |impl Needs {
   25|       |    /// Returns the worst (lowest) need value.
   26|       |    #[must_use]
   27|      3|    pub const fn worst(&self) -> f32 {
   28|      3|        let min_hr = if self.hunger < self.rest {
   29|      1|            self.hunger
   30|       |        } else {
   31|      2|            self.rest
   32|       |        };
   33|      3|        if min_hr < self.leisure {
   34|      2|            min_hr
   35|       |        } else {
   36|      1|            self.leisure
   37|       |        }
   38|      3|    }
   39|       |
   40|       |    /// Calculates aggregate morale score (0.0 to 1.0).
   41|       |    #[must_use]
   42|     52|    pub fn morale(&self) -> f32 {
   43|     52|        (self.hunger + self.rest + self.leisure) / 3.0
   44|     52|    }
   45|       |}
   46|       |
   47|       |/// Returns work efficiency multiplier based on morale.
   48|       |#[must_use]
   49|     54|pub fn get_morale_efficiency(morale: f32) -> f32 {
   50|     54|    if morale >= 0.8 {
   51|     42|        1.2
   52|     12|    } else if morale <= 0.2 {
   53|      3|        0.5
   54|       |    } else {
   55|      9|        1.0
   56|       |    }
   57|     54|}
   58|       |
   59|       |const HUNGER_DECAY_PER_TICK: f32 = 0.001; // ~800 ticks to starve from full
   60|       |const REST_DECAY_PER_TICK: f32 = 0.001; // ~800 ticks to exhaust
   61|       |const LEISURE_DECAY_PER_TICK: f32 = 0.0015; // Slightly faster than hunger/rest
   62|       |
   63|       |/// Decays needs for all pops each tick.
   64|       |///
   65|       |/// Uses `par_iter_mut` for parallel processing across entities.
   66|    115|pub fn decay_needs_system(mut query: Query<&mut Needs>) {
   67|    163|    query.par_iter_mut().for_each(|mut needs| {
                  ^115  ^115           ^115
   68|    163|        needs.hunger = (needs.hunger - HUNGER_DECAY_PER_TICK).max(0.0);
   69|    163|        needs.rest = (needs.rest - REST_DECAY_PER_TICK).max(0.0);
   70|    163|        needs.leisure = (needs.leisure - LEISURE_DECAY_PER_TICK).max(0.0);
   71|    163|    });
   72|    115|}
   73|       |

   95|      1|            hunger: 0.5,

    7|       |    /// Informational message (blue/neutral).
    8|       |    Info,

   10|       |    Success,

   17|       |/// A temporary alert displayed to the player.
   18|       |#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
   19|       |pub struct Notification {
   20|       |    /// Unique identifier.
   21|       |    pub id: u64,
   22|       |    /// The message text.
   23|       |    pub text: String,
   24|       |    /// Severity level.
   25|       |    pub severity: NotificationSeverity,
   26|       |    /// Tick when created.

   28|       |    /// Tick when it expires (if any).
   29|       |    pub expires_at: Option<u64>,

   32|       |}
   33|       |
   34|       |/// Resource managing the queue of active notifications.
   35|       |#[derive(Resource, Default)]
   36|       |pub struct NotificationQueue {
   37|       |    /// List of active notifications.
   38|       |    pub active: Vec<Notification>,
   39|       |    next_id: u64,
   40|       |}
   41|       |
   42|       |impl NotificationQueue {
   43|       |    const MAX_NOTIFICATIONS: usize = 10;
   44|       |
   45|       |    /// Adds a notification with default expiration (300 ticks).
   46|     17|    pub fn add(&mut self, text: String, severity: NotificationSeverity, current_tick: u64) -> u64 {
   47|       |        // Default expiration: 300 ticks (30s at 10TPS)
   48|     17|        self.add_with_expiration(text, severity, current_tick, current_tick + 300)
   49|     17|    }
   50|       |
   51|       |    /// Adds a notification with explicit expiration.
   52|     18|    pub fn add_with_expiration(
   53|     18|        &mut self,
   54|     18|        text: String,
   55|     18|        severity: NotificationSeverity,
   56|     18|        current_tick: u64,
   57|     18|        expires_at: u64
   58|     18|    ) -> u64 {
   59|     18|        let id = self.next_id;
   60|     18|        self.next_id += 1;
   61|       |
   62|     18|        let notification = Notification {
   63|     18|            id,
   64|     18|            text,
   65|     18|            severity,
   66|     18|            created_at: current_tick,
   67|     18|            expires_at: Some(expires_at),
   68|     18|            action_link: None,
   69|     18|        };
   70|       |
   71|     18|        self.active.push(notification);
   72|       |
   73|       |        // Enforce limit (FIFO)
   74|     18|        if self.active.len() > Self::MAX_NOTIFICATIONS {
   75|      5|            self.active.remove(0);
   76|     13|        }
   77|       |
   78|     18|        id
   79|     18|    }
   80|       |
   81|       |    /// Dismisses a notification by ID.
   82|      1|    pub fn dismiss(&mut self, id: u64) {
   83|      1|        if let Some(pos) = self.active.iter().position(|n| n.id == id) {
   84|      1|            self.active.remove(pos);
   85|      1|        }
                      ^0
   86|      1|    }
   87|       |}
   88|       |
   89|       |/// System that removes expired notifications.
   90|     14|pub fn notification_expiration_system(
   91|     14|    mut queue: ResMut<NotificationQueue>,
   92|     14|    time: Res<SimulationTime>,
   93|     14|) {
   94|     14|    let current_tick = time.tick;
   95|     14|    queue.active.retain(|n| n.expires_at.is_none_or(|expiry| current_tick < expiry));
                                          ^2           ^2                  ^2             ^2
   96|     14|}

/app/src/layer1/pop.rs:
    1|       |//! Population management and entity definitions.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! # The "Pop" (Population Agent)
    4|       |//!
    5|       |//! A "Pop" is the atomic unit of agency in the colony. They are not just resources;
    6|       |//! they are semi-autonomous agents driven by a hierarchy of needs (see `needs.rs`)
    7|       |//! and decision-making logic (see `utility_ai.rs`).
    8|       |//!
    9|       |//! ## Lifecycle
   10|       |//!
   11|       |//! 1.  **Spawning**: Pops are created by `spawn_initial_pops` (or potential future immigration events).
   12|       |//! 2.  **Simulation**: Every tick, systems in `needs.rs` update their physiological state.
   13|       |//! 3.  **Decision**: The Utility AI (`utility_ai.rs`) evaluates options and assigns a `PopAction`.
   14|       |//! 4.  **Execution**: The chosen action is carried out, modifying the world or the pop's state.
   15|       |//!
   16|       |//! ## Components
   17|       |//!
   18|       |//! A fully initialized Pop entity typically has:
   19|       |//! * `Pop`: The marker component.
   20|       |//! * `GridPosition`: Physical location.
   21|       |//! * `Needs`: Hunger, rest, etc.
   22|       |//! * `PopAction`: Current task state.
   23|       |//! * `UtilityWeights`: Personality/learning factors.
   24|       |
   25|       |use super::health::Health;
   26|       |use super::map::GridPosition;
   27|       |use super::memory::Memories;
   28|       |use super::needs::Needs;
   29|       |use super::terrain::{TerrainGrid, TerrainType};
   30|       |use super::utility_ai::{PopAction, UtilityWeights};
   31|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   32|       |use rand::Rng;
   33|       |
   34|       |/// Marker component for pop entities.
   35|       |///
   36|       |/// This component identifies an entity as a "Citizen" of the colony. It is the
   37|       |/// primary query filter for most simulation systems (needs, AI, jobs).
   38|       |///
   39|       |/// # Examples
   40|       |///
   41|       |/// Querying all pops:
   42|       |/// ```
   43|       |/// use scale::layer1::pop::Pop;
   44|       |/// use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   45|       |///
   46|       |/// fn count_pops(query: Query<&Pop>) -> usize {
   47|       |///     query.iter().count()
   48|       |/// }
   49|       |/// ```
   50|       |#[derive(Component)]
   51|       |pub struct Pop;
   52|       |
   53|       |/// Spawn 5 initial pops at random walkable positions.
   54|       |///
   55|       |/// This function attempts to find valid starting locations for the initial colony.
   56|       |/// It will retry random coordinates until it finds a tile that is:
   57|       |/// * Within bounds
   58|       |/// * Not Water
   59|       |/// * Not Rock
   60|       |///
   61|       |/// If it fails to find a spot after `MAX_ATTEMPTS` (1000), it gives up for that pop.
   62|     14|pub fn spawn_initial_pops(world: &mut World) {
   63|     14|    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
   64|     14|    spawn_initial_pops_internal(world, &mut rng);
   65|     14|}
   66|       |
   67|     18|fn spawn_initial_pops_internal<R: Rng>(world: &mut World, rng: &mut R) {
   68|       |    const MAX_ATTEMPTS: usize = 1000;
   69|       |
   70|       |    // Get dimensions first to release borrow
   71|     18|    let (width, height) = {
   72|     18|        let terrain = world.resource::<TerrainGrid>();
   73|     18|        (terrain.width, terrain.height)
   74|     18|    };
   75|       |
   76|       |    // Safe bounds for GridPosition (i32)
   77|       |    // If map is larger than i32::MAX, we just spawn within the i32 limit
   78|       |    // because GridPosition cannot represent coordinates beyond that anyway.
   79|     18|    let max_x = i32::try_from(width).unwrap_or(i32::MAX);
   80|     18|    let max_y = i32::try_from(height).unwrap_or(i32::MAX);
   81|       |
   82|     18|    let mut spawned = 0;
   83|     18|    let mut attempts = 0;
   84|       |    // Safety: we assume there is at least one walkable tile to avoid infinite loop.
   85|       |    // In a real game we might want a timeout or more robust search.
   86|  2.07k|    while spawned < 5 {
   87|  2.05k|        attempts += 1;
   88|  2.05k|        if attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS {
   89|      1|            break;
   90|  2.05k|        }
   91|       |
   92|  2.05k|        let x = rng.gen_range(0..max_x);
   93|  2.05k|        let y = rng.gen_range(0..max_y);
   94|       |
   95|       |        // Check terrain type in a separate scope to handle borrowing
   96|  2.05k|        let is_walkable = {
   97|  2.05k|            let terrain = world.resource::<TerrainGrid>();
   98|       |            #[allow(clippy::cast_sign_loss)]
   99|  2.05k|            terrain
  100|  2.05k|                .get(x as usize, y as usize)
  101|  2.05k|                .is_some_and(|t| t != TerrainType::Water && t != TerrainType::Rock)
                                                                          ^94
  102|       |        };
  103|       |
  104|  2.05k|        if is_walkable {
  105|     85|            world.spawn((
  106|     85|                Pop,
  107|     85|                GridPosition { x, y },
  108|     85|                Health::default(),
  109|     85|                Needs::default(),
  110|     85|                Memories::default(),
  111|     85|                PopAction::default(),
  112|     85|                UtilityWeights::default(),
  113|     85|            ));
  114|     85|            spawned += 1;
  115|  1.97k|        }
  116|       |    }
  117|     18|}
  118|       |

  359|       |        count: usize,
  360|       |        limit: usize,
  361|       |    }
  362|       |
  363|       |    impl RngCore for LimitedRng {
  364|  1.99k|        fn next_u32(&mut self) -> u32 {
  365|       |            #[allow(clippy::cast_possible_truncation)]
  366|       |            {
  367|  1.99k|                self.next_u64() as u32
  368|       |            }
  369|  1.99k|        }
  370|       |
  371|  1.99k|        fn next_u64(&mut self) -> u64 {
  372|  1.99k|            self.count += 1;
  373|  1.99k|            assert!(
  374|  1.99k|                self.count <= self.limit,
  375|      0|                "Too many RNG calls - infinite loop detected"
  376|       |            );
  377|  1.99k|            0
  378|  1.99k|        }
  379|       |
  380|      0|        fn fill_bytes(&mut self, dest: &mut [u8]) {
  381|      0|            for b in dest {
  382|      0|                *b = 0;
  383|      0|            }
  384|      0|            self.next_u64();
  385|      0|        }
  386|       |
  387|      0|        fn try_fill_bytes(&mut self, dest: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(), rand::Error> {
  388|      0|            self.fill_bytes(dest);
  389|      0|            Ok(())
  390|      0|        }

    3|       |
    4|       |use crate::layer1::GridPosition;
    5|       |use crate::layer1::building::{Building, BuildingType};
    6|       |use crate::layer1::pop::Pop;
    7|       |use crate::layer1::resources::{ColonyResources, RefiningProgress};
    8|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
    9|       |
   10|       |/// System that processes refining at buildings like Lumber Mills and Stone Masons.
   11|       |///
   12|       |/// It iterates over buildings with `RefiningProgress`. If a worker (`Pop`) is nearby
   13|       |/// and input resources are available, it increments progress. Upon completion,
   14|       |/// it consumes input resources and produces refined resources.
   15|     18|pub fn process_refining_system(
   16|     18|    worker_query: Query<&GridPosition, With<Pop>>,
   17|     18|    mut building_query: Query<(Entity, &Building, &GridPosition, &mut RefiningProgress)>,
   18|     18|    mut resources: ResMut<ColonyResources>,
   19|     18|) {
   20|     18|    let worker_positions: Vec<GridPosition> = worker_query.iter().copied().collect();
   21|       |
   22|       |    // Snapshot resources for recipe checks (avoids mutable borrow conflict)
   23|     18|    let resources_snapshot = resources.clone();
   24|       |
   25|     18|    let mut finished_jobs: Vec<(Entity, ColonyResources, ColonyResources)> = Vec::new();
   26|       |
   27|     25|    for (entity, building, pos, mut progress) in &mut building_query {
                       ^7      ^7        ^7   ^7
   28|      7|        let has_worker = worker_positions
   29|      7|            .iter()
   30|      7|            .any(|p| (p.x - pos.x).abs() + (p.y - pos.y).abs() <= 10);
   31|       |
   32|      7|        if !has_worker {
   33|      0|            continue;
   34|      7|        }
   35|       |
   36|      7|        let (can_refine, input_cost, output_gain) =
   37|      7|            get_refining_recipe(building.building_type, &resources_snapshot);
   38|       |
   39|      7|        if can_refine {
   40|      5|            progress.current += 1.0;
   41|      5|            if progress.is_complete() {
   42|      4|                finished_jobs.push((entity, input_cost, output_gain));
   43|      4|            }
                          ^1
   44|      2|        }
   45|       |    }
   46|       |
   47|       |    // Apply resource updates for finished jobs
   48|     22|    for (entity, input, output) in &finished_jobs {
                       ^4      ^4     ^4
   49|      4|        if resources.try_deduct(input) {
   50|      3|            resources.add_planks(output.planks);
   51|      3|            resources.add_blocks(output.blocks);
   52|      3|            resources.add_metal(output.metal);
   53|      3|            resources.add_tools(output.tools);
   54|      3|            resources.add_cloth(output.cloth);
   55|      3|            resources.add_clothing(output.clothing);
   56|       |
   57|      3|            if let Ok((_, _, _, mut progress)) = building_query.get_mut(*entity) {
   58|      3|                progress.current = 0.0;
   59|      3|            }
                          ^0
   60|       |        } else {
   61|       |            // Failed: clamp progress to max for retry next tick
   62|      1|            if let Ok((_, _, _, mut progress)) = building_query.get_mut(*entity) {
   63|      1|                progress.current = progress.max;
   64|      1|            }
                          ^0
   65|       |        }
   66|       |    }
   67|     18|}

   69|       |/// Returns the refining recipe for a building type.
   70|       |///
   71|       |/// # Returns
   72|       |/// (`can_afford`, `input_cost`, `output_gain`)
   73|       |#[must_use]
   74|     10|pub fn get_refining_recipe(
   75|     10|    building_type: BuildingType,
   76|     10|    res: &ColonyResources,
   77|     10|) -> (bool, ColonyResources, ColonyResources) {
   78|     10|    match building_type {
   79|       |        BuildingType::LumberMill => (
   80|      6|            res.wood >= 1.0 && res.planks < res.max_planks,
                                             ^5
   81|      6|            ColonyResources {
   82|      6|                wood: 1.0,
   83|      6|                ..Default::default()
   84|      6|            },
   85|      6|            ColonyResources {
   86|      6|                planks: 1.0,
   87|      6|                ..Default::default()
   88|      6|            },
   89|       |        ),
   90|       |        BuildingType::StoneMason => (
   91|      0|            res.stone >= 1.0 && res.blocks < res.max_blocks,
   92|      0|            ColonyResources {
   93|      0|                stone: 1.0,
   94|      0|                ..Default::default()
   95|      0|            },
   96|      0|            ColonyResources {
   97|      0|                blocks: 1.0,
   98|      0|                ..Default::default()
   99|      0|            },
  100|       |        ),
  101|       |        BuildingType::Smelter => (
  102|      1|            res.ore >= 1.0 && res.wood >= 1.0 && res.metal < res.max_metal,
  103|      1|            ColonyResources {
  104|      1|                ore: 1.0,
  105|      1|                wood: 1.0,
  106|      1|                ..Default::default()
  107|      1|            },
  108|      1|            ColonyResources {
  109|      1|                metal: 1.0,
  110|      1|                ..Default::default()
  111|      1|            },
  112|       |        ),
  113|       |        BuildingType::Smithy => (
  114|      1|            res.metal >= 1.0 && res.wood >= 1.0 && res.tools < res.max_tools,
  115|      1|            ColonyResources {
  116|      1|                metal: 1.0,
  117|      1|                wood: 1.0,
  118|      1|                ..Default::default()
  119|      1|            },
  120|      1|            ColonyResources {
  121|      1|                tools: 1.0,
  122|      1|                ..Default::default()
  123|      1|            },
  124|       |        ),
  125|       |        BuildingType::Weaver => (
  126|      1|            res.fiber >= 1.0 && res.cloth < res.max_cloth,
  127|      1|            ColonyResources {
  128|      1|                fiber: 1.0,
  129|      1|                ..Default::default()
  130|      1|            },
  131|      1|            ColonyResources {
  132|      1|                cloth: 1.0,
  133|      1|                ..Default::default()
  134|      1|            },
  135|       |        ),
  136|       |        BuildingType::Tailor => (
  137|      1|            res.cloth >= 1.0 && res.clothing < res.max_clothing,
  138|      1|            ColonyResources {
  139|      1|                cloth: 1.0,
  140|      1|                ..Default::default()
  141|      1|            },
  142|      1|            ColonyResources {
  143|      1|                clothing: 1.0,
  144|      1|                ..Default::default()
  145|      1|            },
  146|       |        ),
  147|      0|        _ => (
  148|      0|            false,
  149|      0|            ColonyResources::default(),
  150|      0|            ColonyResources::default(),
  151|      0|        ),
  152|       |    }

/app/src/layer1/resources.rs:
    1|       |//! Resource management and mining mechanics.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! This module defines the colony's economic backbone: `ColonyResources` and the
    4|       |//! mechanisms to extract them from the environment (e.g., `mine_rock`).
    5|       |//!
    6|       |//! # Key Concepts
    7|       |//!
    8|       |//! * **ColonyResources**: The global stockpile of Food, Wood, and Stone.
    9|       |//! * **Mining**: A multi-tick process tracked by `MiningProgress` that converts
   10|       |//!   terrain (Rock -> Dirt) and yields resources (Stone).
   11|       |//!
   12|       |//! # The Mining Loop
   13|       |//!
   14|       |//! 1. Player designates a tile (see `crate::layer1::designation`).
   15|       |//! 2. A pop is assigned the job (see `crate::layer1::pop`).
   16|       |//! 3. The pop works on the tile, calling `mine_rock`.
   17|       |//! 4. `MiningProgress` accumulates.
   18|       |//! 5. Upon completion, the tile changes and resources are awarded.
   19|       |
   20|       |use crate::layer1::GridPosition;
   21|       |use crate::layer1::terrain::{TerrainGrid, TerrainType};
   22|       |use crate::shared::log::MessageLog;
   23|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   24|       |use rand::Rng;
   25|       |

   29|       |    /// Consumed by pops to reduce hunger.
   30|       |    Food,

  254|       |    /// Checks if the colony can afford the given cost.
  255|       |    ///
  256|       |    /// # Parameters
  257|       |    ///
  258|       |    /// * `cost`: The resources required.
  259|       |    ///
  260|       |    /// # Returns
  261|       |    ///
  262|       |    /// True if all resources are sufficient.
  263|       |    #[must_use]
  264|     19|    pub fn can_afford(&self, cost: &Self) -> bool {
  265|     19|        self.food >= cost.food
  266|     18|            && self.wood >= cost.wood
  267|     15|            && self.stone >= cost.stone
  268|     15|            && self.planks >= cost.planks
  269|     15|            && self.blocks >= cost.blocks
  270|     15|            && self.ore >= cost.ore
  271|     15|            && self.metal >= cost.metal
  272|     15|            && self.tools >= cost.tools
  273|     15|            && self.knowledge >= cost.knowledge
  274|     15|            && self.fiber >= cost.fiber
  275|     15|            && self.cloth >= cost.cloth
  276|     15|            && self.clothing >= cost.clothing
  277|     19|    }

  279|       |    /// Deducts the given cost from the colony's resources.
  280|       |    ///
  281|       |    /// # Parameters
  282|       |    ///
  283|       |    /// * `cost`: The resources to deduct.
  284|     14|    pub fn deduct(&mut self, cost: &Self) {
  285|     14|        self.food -= cost.food;
  286|     14|        self.wood -= cost.wood;
  287|     14|        self.stone -= cost.stone;
  288|     14|        self.planks -= cost.planks;
  289|     14|        self.blocks -= cost.blocks;
  290|     14|        self.ore -= cost.ore;
  291|     14|        self.metal -= cost.metal;
  292|     14|        self.tools -= cost.tools;
  293|     14|        self.knowledge -= cost.knowledge;
  294|     14|        self.fiber -= cost.fiber;
  295|     14|        self.cloth -= cost.cloth;
  296|     14|        self.clothing -= cost.clothing;
  297|     14|    }

  299|       |    /// Attempts to deduct the given cost from the colony's resources.
  300|       |    ///
  301|       |    /// Checks affordability first. If affordable, deducts and returns `true`.
  302|       |    /// Otherwise, returns `false` and makes no changes.

  304|       |    /// # Parameters
  305|       |    ///
  306|       |    /// * `cost`: The resources to deduct.
  307|      6|    pub fn try_deduct(&mut self, cost: &Self) -> bool {
  308|      6|        if self.can_afford(cost) {
  309|      4|            self.deduct(cost);
  310|      4|            true
  311|       |        } else {

  313|       |        }

  395|       |
  396|       |/// Applies work to a mining designation.
  397|       |///
  398|       |/// This function is the core of the mining mechanic. It advances the `MiningProgress`
  399|       |/// of a specific designation. If the work completes the task, it:
  400|       |/// 1. Despawns the designation.
  401|       |/// 2. Changes the terrain from `Rock` to `Dirt`.
  402|       |/// 3. Spawns `Stone` as a `ResourceItem` (must be hauled).
  403|       |///
  404|       |/// # Parameters
  405|       |///
  406|       |/// * `world`: Mutable access to the ECS world (needed to modify terrain and resources).
  407|       |/// * `designation_entity`: The entity ID of the designation being worked on.
  408|       |/// * `work_amount`: How much progress to add (usually based on worker skill/speed).
  409|       |///
  410|       |/// # Examples
  411|       |///
  412|       |/// ```
  413|       |/// use scale::layer1::resources::{mine_rock, ColonyResources, MiningProgress, ResourceItem, ResourceType};
  414|       |/// use scale::layer1::terrain::{TerrainGrid, TerrainType};
  415|       |/// use scale::layer1::GridPosition;
  416|       |/// use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
  417|       |///
  418|       |/// let mut world = World::new();
  419|       |///
  420|       |/// // 1. Setup World
  421|       |/// let mut tiles = vec![TerrainType::Grass; 100];
  422|       |/// tiles[0] = TerrainType::Rock; // Target is rock
  423|       |/// world.insert_resource(TerrainGrid { width: 10, height: 10, tiles });
  424|       |/// world.insert_resource(ColonyResources::default());
  425|       |///
  426|       |/// // 2. Create Designation
  427|       |/// let designation = world.spawn((
  428|       |///     GridPosition { x: 0, y: 0 },
  429|       |///     MiningProgress { current: 0.0, max: 10.0 }
  430|       |/// )).id();
  431|       |///
  432|       |/// // 3. Work until done
  433|       |/// mine_rock(&mut world, designation, 10.0);
  434|       |///
  435|       |/// // 4. Verify Result
  436|       |/// // The resource is spawned as an item on the ground, not added directly to stocks.
  437|       |/// let items: Vec<_> = world.query::<&ResourceItem>().iter(&world).collect();
  438|       |/// assert!(!items.is_empty());
  439|       |/// assert_eq!(items[0].resource_type, ResourceType::Stone);
  440|       |/// ```

    1|       |use crate::layer1::building::OccupiedTiles;

    7|       |use crate::shared::log::MessageLog;
    8|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
    9|       |use rand::Rng;
   10|       |
   11|       |/// Type of anomaly found on the map.
   12|       |#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Component)]
   13|       |pub enum AnomalyType {
   14|       |    /// Ancient ruins containing knowledge.
   15|       |    Ruins,

   58|       |/// Tracks the current season of the colony.
   59|       |#[derive(Resource, Default)]
   60|       |pub struct SeasonState {
   61|       |    /// The current active season.
   62|       |    pub current_season: Season,
   63|       |}
   64|       |
   65|       |/// Updates the current season based on the simulation tick.
   66|       |///
   67|       |/// This system calculates the season by dividing the current tick by `TICKS_PER_YEAR / 4`.
   68|       |/// It updates `SeasonState` and adds a `Chronicle` event when the season changes.
   69|     15|pub fn advance_season_system(
   70|     15|    time: Res<SimulationTime>,
   71|     15|    mut state: ResMut<SeasonState>,
   72|     15|    mut chronicle: ResMut<Chronicle>,
   73|     15|) {
   74|     15|    let tick = time.tick;
   75|     15|    let ticks_per_season = TICKS_PER_YEAR / 4;
   76|       |
   77|     15|    let season_index = (tick / ticks_per_season) % 4;
   78|     15|    let new_season = match season_index {
   79|     13|        0 => Season::Spring,
   80|      2|        1 => Season::Summer,
   81|      0|        2 => Season::Autumn,
   82|      0|        _ => Season::Winter,
   83|       |    };
   84|       |
   85|     15|    if state.current_season != new_season {
   86|      1|        state.current_season = new_season;
   87|      1|        chronicle.add_event(
   88|      1|            tick,
   89|      1|            format!("The season turns. {} has arrived.", new_season.name()),
   90|      1|            EventImportance::Standard,
   91|      1|        );
   92|     14|    }

   15|       |    pub visitors: Vec<Entity>,
   16|       |}

   19|      4|    fn default() -> Self {
   20|      4|        Self {
   21|      4|            capacity: 5,
   22|      4|            visitors: Vec::new(),
   23|      4|        }
   24|      4|    }

   26|       |
   27|       |/// Evaluates the utility of socializing at available taverns.
   28|     13|pub fn evaluate_socialize<'a>(
   29|     13|    pop_pos: &GridPosition,
   30|     13|    needs: &Needs,
   31|     13|    weights: &UtilityWeights,
   32|     13|    taverns: impl Iterator<Item = (Entity, &'a GridPosition, &'a Tavern)>,
   33|     13|) -> Option<(f32, Entity)> {
   34|     13|    let urgency = need_response_curve(needs.leisure);
   35|     13|    let mut best: Option<(f32, Entity)> = None;
   36|       |
   37|     15|    for (entity, pos, tavern) in taverns {
                       ^2      ^2   ^2
   38|      2|        let context = calculate_context_score(
   39|      2|            *pop_pos,
   40|      2|            Some(*pos),
   41|      2|            tavern.capacity,
   42|      2|            tavern.visitors.len(),
   43|      2|            weights,
   44|       |        );
   45|       |
   46|      2|        let success = calculate_success_modifier(ActionType::Socialize, weights);
   47|      2|        let utility = urgency * context * success;
   48|       |
   49|      2|        if best.is_none_or(|(u, _)| utility > u) {
                                                  ^0        ^0
   50|      2|            best = Some((utility, entity));
   51|      2|        }
                      ^0
   52|       |    }
   53|     13|    best
   54|     13|}
   55|       |
   56|       |/// Restores leisure for pops visiting taverns.
   57|     13|pub fn restore_leisure_system(tavern_query: Query<&Tavern>, mut needs_query: Query<&mut Needs>) {
   58|     14|    for tavern in &tavern_query {
                      ^1
   59|      2|        for &visitor in &tavern.visitors {
                           ^1
   60|      1|            if let Ok(mut needs) = needs_query.get_mut(visitor) {
   61|      1|                needs.leisure = (needs.leisure + 0.05).min(1.0);
   62|      1|            }
                          ^0
   63|       |        }
   64|       |    }

    2|       |use crate::layer1::resources::ColonyResources;

    4|       |
    5|       |/// Component indicating an item can spoil/rot over time.
    6|       |#[derive(Component, Debug, Clone, Copy)]
    7|       |pub struct Perishable {
    8|       |    /// Number of ticks since creation/spawn.
    9|       |    pub current_ticks: u32,
   10|       |    /// Maximum ticks before the item is destroyed.
   11|       |    pub max_ticks: u32,
   12|       |}
   13|       |
   14|       |impl Default for Perishable {
   15|      1|    fn default() -> Self {
   16|      1|        Self {
   17|      1|            current_ticks: 0,
   18|      1|            #[allow(clippy::cast_possible_truncation)]
   19|      1|            max_ticks: TICKS_PER_YEAR as u32,
   20|      1|        }
   21|      1|    }
   22|       |}
   23|       |
   24|       |/// Percentage of global food that spoils per tick (0.05%).
   25|       |pub const GLOBAL_SPOILAGE_RATE: f32 = 0.0005;
   26|       |
   27|       |/// System that handles decay of `ColonyResources` (food) and `Perishable` entities.
   28|     20|pub fn spoilage_system(
   29|     20|    mut commands: Commands,
   30|     20|    mut resources: ResMut<ColonyResources>,
   31|     20|    mut query: Query<(Entity, &mut Perishable)>,
   32|     20|) {
   33|       |    // 1. Handle Global Spoilage
   34|     20|    if resources.food > 0.0 {
   35|     20|        let decay = resources.food * GLOBAL_SPOILAGE_RATE;
   36|     20|        resources.food = (resources.food - decay).max(0.0);
   37|     20|    }
                  ^0
   38|       |
   39|       |    // 2. Handle Perishable Items
   40|     26|    for (entity, mut perishable) in &mut query {
                       ^6      ^6
   41|      6|        perishable.current_ticks += 1;
   42|      6|        if perishable.current_ticks >= perishable.max_ticks {
   43|      1|            commands.entity(entity).despawn();
   44|      5|        }
   45|       |    }
   46|     20|}

    4|       |/// Component that increases the maximum resource capacity of the colony.

    7|       |    /// Bonus to max food capacity.
    8|       |    pub food_bonus: f32,

   10|       |    pub wood_bonus: f32,

   12|       |    pub stone_bonus: f32,
   13|       |}
   14|       |
   15|       |impl Default for Stockpile {
   16|      6|    fn default() -> Self {
   17|      6|        Self {
   18|      6|            food_bonus: 0.0,
   19|      6|            wood_bonus: 100.0,
   20|      6|            stone_bonus: 100.0,
   21|      6|        }
   22|      6|    }
   23|       |}
   24|       |
   25|       |/// System that calculates and updates the colony's resource caps based on existing stockpiles.
   26|     13|pub fn update_resource_caps_system(
   27|     13|    query: Query<&Stockpile>,
   28|     13|    mut resources: ResMut<ColonyResources>,
   29|     13|) {
   30|     13|    let mut total_food_bonus = 0.0;
   31|     13|    let mut total_wood_bonus = 0.0;
   32|     13|    let mut total_stone_bonus = 0.0;
   33|       |
   34|     15|    for stockpile in &query {
                      ^2
   35|      2|        total_food_bonus += stockpile.food_bonus;
   36|      2|        total_wood_bonus += stockpile.wood_bonus;
   37|      2|        total_stone_bonus += stockpile.stone_bonus;
   38|      2|    }
   39|       |
   40|     13|    resources.max_food = BASE_MAX_FOOD + total_food_bonus;
   41|     13|    resources.max_wood = BASE_MAX_WOOD + total_wood_bonus;
   42|     13|    resources.max_stone = BASE_MAX_STONE + total_stone_bonus;
   43|       |
   44|     13|    resources.food = resources.food.min(resources.max_food);
   45|     13|    resources.wood = resources.wood.min(resources.max_wood);
   46|     13|    resources.stone = resources.stone.min(resources.max_stone);
   47|     13|}

   16|     11|    fn default() -> Self {
   17|     11|        Self {

   19|     11|            max_hp: 100.0,
   20|     11|        }

   23|       |
   24|       |/// System that applies fire damage to structures.
   25|       |///
   26|       |/// This runs every tick and reduces HP of structures standing in fire.
   27|       |/// If HP reaches 0, the structure is destroyed.
   28|     14|pub fn fire_damage_structure_system(world: &mut World) {
   29|     14|    let mut destroyed = Vec::new();
   30|       |
   31|       |    // Query for fires first to avoid borrowing conflict
   32|     14|    let mut fires = Vec::new();
   33|     14|    let mut fire_query = world.query::<(&GridPosition, &Fire)>();
   34|     14|    for (pos, fire) in fire_query.iter(world) {
                       ^2   ^2
   35|      2|        fires.push((*pos, fire.intensity));
   36|      2|    }
   37|       |
   38|       |    // Apply damage to structures at fire locations
   39|       |    // This is O(F * S) which is fine for MVP. Optimization: Spatial Map.
   40|     16|    for (fire_pos, intensity) in fires {
                       ^2        ^2
   41|      2|        let mut structure_query = world.query::<(Entity, &GridPosition, &mut Structure)>();
   42|      2|        for (entity, pos, mut structure) in structure_query.iter_mut(world) {
   43|      2|            if *pos == fire_pos {
   44|      2|                let damage = 5.0 * intensity; // 5.0 damage per tick per intensity unit
   45|      2|                structure.current_hp -= damage;
   46|       |
   47|      2|                if structure.current_hp <= 0.0 {
   48|      1|                    destroyed.push((entity, *pos));
   49|      1|                }
   50|      0|            }
   51|       |        }
   52|       |    }
   53|       |
   54|       |    // Despawn destroyed structures
   55|     15|    for (entity, pos) in destroyed {
                       ^1      ^1
   56|       |        // Double check it still exists (though unlikely to change within loop)
   57|      1|        if world.get_entity(entity).is_ok() {
   58|      1|            world.despawn(entity);
   59|       |            // Clean up OccupiedTiles
   60|      1|            if let Some(mut occupied) = world.get_resource_mut::<OccupiedTiles>() {
                                      ^0
   61|      0|                occupied.0.remove(&(pos.x, pos.y));
   62|      1|            }
   63|       |
   64|       |            // Add log message
   65|      1|            if let Some(mut log) = world.get_resource_mut::<crate::shared::log::MessageLog>() {
                                      ^0
   66|      0|                log.add(format!(
   67|      0|                    "Structure destroyed by fire at ({}, {})",
   68|      0|                    pos.x, pos.y
   69|      0|                ));
   70|      1|            }
   71|      0|        }
   72|       |    }
   73|     14|}
   74|       |
   75|       |/// Logic to process repair work.
   76|       |///
   77|       |/// Increases structure HP and removes designation if fully repaired.
   78|      2|pub fn process_repair(world: &mut World, designation_entity: Entity, amount: f32) {
   79|       |    // 1. Get designation position
   80|      2|    let Some(pos) = world.get::<GridPosition>(designation_entity).copied() else {
   81|      0|        return;
   82|       |    };
   83|       |
   84|       |    // 2. Find structure at that position
   85|      2|    let mut structure_entity = None;
   86|      2|    let mut new_hp = 0.0;
   87|      2|    let mut max_hp = 0.0;
   88|       |
   89|       |    // Use a scope to borrow world for query
   90|       |    {
   91|      2|        let mut query = world.query::<(Entity, &GridPosition, &mut Structure)>();
   92|      2|        for (entity, p, mut s) in query.iter_mut(world) {
   93|      2|            if *p == pos {
   94|      2|                s.current_hp = (s.current_hp + amount).min(s.max_hp);
   95|      2|                new_hp = s.current_hp;
   96|      2|                max_hp = s.max_hp;
   97|      2|                structure_entity = Some(entity);
   98|      2|                break;
   99|      0|            }
  100|       |        }
  101|       |    }
  102|       |
  103|       |    // 3. If fully repaired, remove designation
  104|      2|    if let Some(_entity) = structure_entity {
  105|      2|        if (new_hp - max_hp).abs() < f32::EPSILON {
  106|      1|            world.despawn(designation_entity);
  107|      1|        }
  108|      0|    } else {
  109|      0|        // If no structure found (destroyed?), remove designation
  110|      0|        world.despawn(designation_entity);
  111|      0|    }
  112|      2|}

   64|       |/// Attempts to unlock a technology using Knowledge.
   65|       |///
   66|       |/// Returns `true` if successful (affordable and not already unlocked, or already unlocked).
   67|       |/// Deducts Knowledge from `ColonyResources`.
   68|      2|pub fn unlock_tech(world: &mut World, tech: Tech) -> bool {
   69|       |    // Check if already unlocked?
   70|      2|    if world.resource::<TechState>().is_unlocked(tech) {
   71|      0|        return true;
   72|      2|    }
   73|       |
   74|      2|    let cost = tech.cost();
   75|      2|    let can_afford = {
   76|      2|        let res = world.resource::<ColonyResources>();
   77|      2|        res.knowledge >= cost
   78|       |    };
   79|       |
   80|      2|    if can_afford {
   81|      1|        world.resource_mut::<ColonyResources>().knowledge -= cost;
   82|      1|        world.resource_mut::<TechState>().unlock(tech);
   83|       |
   84|      1|        if let Some(mut log) = world.get_resource_mut::<MessageLog>() {
   85|      1|            log.add(format!("Researched: {}", tech.label()));
   86|      1|        }
                      ^0
   87|      1|        true
   88|       |    } else {
   89|      1|        false
   90|       |    }

   93|       |/// Generates knowledge based on pops working at Libraries.
   94|     12|pub fn process_research_system(
   95|     12|    pops: Query<&AssignedTo>,
   96|     12|    libraries: Query<Entity, With<Library>>,
   97|     12|    mut resources: ResMut<ColonyResources>,
   98|     12|) {
   99|     12|    let mut library_workers = std::collections::HashMap::<Entity, u32>::new();
  100|       |
  101|     12|    for assignment in &pops {
                      ^0
  102|      0|        if assignment.assignment_type == AssignmentType::LibraryWorker {
  103|      0|            *library_workers.entry(assignment.entity).or_insert(0) += 1;
  104|      0|        }
  105|       |    }
  106|       |
  107|     12|    let mut total_knowledge_gained = 0.0;
  108|       |
  109|     12|    for library_entity in &libraries {
                      ^0
  110|      0|        if let Some(&workers) = library_workers.get(&library_entity) {
  111|      0|            #[allow(clippy::cast_precision_loss)]
  112|      0|            let knowledge_gain = 0.01 * workers as f32;
  113|      0|            total_knowledge_gained += knowledge_gain;
  114|      0|        }
  115|       |    }
  116|       |
  117|     12|    if total_knowledge_gained > 0.0 {
  118|      0|        resources.knowledge =
  119|      0|            (resources.knowledge + total_knowledge_gained).clamp(0.0, resources.max_knowledge);
  120|     12|    }

  137|       |    // 1. Check if any stockpile exists (optimization: no point hauling if nowhere to put it)

  139|     11|        return None;
  140|      2|    }
  141|       |
  142|       |    // 2. Find closest item we have room for
  143|      2|    let mut best: Option<(f32, Entity)> = None;
  144|      2|    let base_utility = 0.6; // Slightly higher than work (0.5) to keep map clean
  145|       |
  146|      4|    for (entity, pos, item) in items {
                       ^2      ^2   ^2
  147|       |        // Check capacity
  148|      2|        let has_room = match item.resource_type {
  149|      0|            crate::layer1::resources::ResourceType::Food => resources.food < resources.max_food,
  150|      0|            crate::layer1::resources::ResourceType::Wood => resources.wood < resources.max_wood,

  158|      0|                resources.blocks < resources.max_blocks
  159|       |            }
  160|       |        };

  162|      2|        if !has_room {
  163|      1|            continue;
  164|      1|        }

  166|      1|        let context = calculate_context_score(
  167|      1|            *pop_pos,
  168|      1|            Some(*pos),
  169|       |            1, // Capacity
  170|       |            0, // Occupied
  171|      1|            weights,
  172|       |        );

                                                       ^0        ^0

  179|      1|        }
                      ^0

  373|       |/// Updates utility weights based on action outcome.
  374|    210|pub fn update_weights_from_outcome(
  375|    210|    weights: &mut UtilityWeights,
  376|    210|    action: ActionType,
  377|    210|    success: bool,
  378|    210|    duration: u32,
  379|    210|    config: &UtilityConfig,
  380|    210|) {
  381|       |    // Track attempt
  382|    210|    let idx = action.as_index();
  383|    210|    weights.action_attempt_count[idx] += 1;
  384|       |
  385|    210|    if success {
  386|    108|        weights.action_success_count[idx] += 1;
  387|       |
  388|       |        // Successful action: reinforce weights
  389|    108|        if duration < 10 {
  390|    101|            weights.distance_weight += config.learning_rate * 0.1;
  391|    101|        }
                      ^7
  392|    108|        weights.availability_weight += config.learning_rate * 0.05;
  393|    102|    } else {
  394|    102|        // Failed action: reduce weights
  395|    102|        weights.distance_weight -= config.learning_rate * 0.05;
  396|    102|        weights.availability_weight -= config.learning_rate * 0.05;
  397|    102|    }
  398|       |
  399|       |    // Clamp weights
  400|    210|    weights.distance_weight = weights
  401|    210|        .distance_weight
  402|    210|        .clamp(config.weight_clamp.0, config.weight_clamp.1);
  403|    210|    weights.availability_weight = weights
  404|    210|        .availability_weight
  405|    210|        .clamp(config.weight_clamp.0, config.weight_clamp.1);
  406|    210|    weights.social_weight = weights
  407|    210|        .social_weight
  408|    210|        .clamp(config.weight_clamp.0, config.weight_clamp.1);

    4|       |/// Calculates urgency from a need value (0.0-1.0).
    5|       |/// Lower need value = higher urgency.
    6|       |#[must_use]
    7|     39|pub fn need_response_curve(need_value: f32) -> f32 {
    8|     39|    need_value.mul_add(-need_value, 1.0)
    9|     39|}
   10|       |
   11|       |/// Calculates Manhattan distance between two positions.
   12|       |#[must_use]
   13|     27|pub const fn manhattan_distance(pos1: &GridPosition, pos2: &GridPosition) -> i32 {
   14|     27|    let dx = (pos1.x as i64 - pos2.x as i64).abs();
   15|     27|    let dy = (pos1.y as i64 - pos2.y as i64).abs();
   16|     27|    let sum = dx + dy;
   17|     27|    if sum > i32::MAX as i64 {
   18|      1|        i32::MAX
   19|       |    } else {
   20|       |        #[allow(clippy::cast_possible_truncation)]
   21|       |        {
   22|     26|            sum as i32
   23|       |        }
   24|       |    }
   25|     27|}
   26|       |
   27|       |/// Calculates a score based on context (distance, availability).
   28|       |#[must_use]
   29|     24|pub fn calculate_context_score(
   30|     24|    pop_pos: GridPosition,
   31|     24|    target_pos: Option<GridPosition>,
   32|     24|    building_capacity: usize,
   33|     24|    building_occupied: usize,
   34|     24|    weights: &UtilityWeights,
   35|     24|) -> f32 {
   36|     24|    let mut score = 1.0;
   37|       |
   38|       |    // Distance factor (closer = better)
   39|     24|    if let Some(target) = target_pos {
   40|     24|        let distance = manhattan_distance(&pop_pos, &target);
   41|     24|        #[allow(clippy::cast_precision_loss)]
   42|     24|        let distance_factor = 1.0 / (distance as f32).mul_add(0.1, 1.0);
   43|     24|        score *= distance_factor.powf(weights.distance_weight);
   44|     24|    }
                  ^0
   45|       |
   46|       |    // Availability factor (less crowded = better)
   47|     24|    if building_capacity > 0 {
   48|     24|        #[allow(clippy::cast_precision_loss)]
   49|     24|        let availability = 1.0 - (building_occupied as f32 / building_capacity as f32);
   50|     24|        score *= availability.powf(weights.availability_weight);
   51|     24|    }
                  ^0
   52|       |
   53|       |    // Social factor (future - for now just identity)
   54|     24|    score *= 1.0_f32.powf(weights.social_weight);
   55|       |
   56|     24|    score.clamp(0.0, 1.0)
   57|     24|}
   58|       |
   59|       |/// Calculates a modifier based on past success rates.
   60|       |#[must_use]
   61|     23|pub fn calculate_success_modifier(action: ActionType, weights: &UtilityWeights) -> f32 {
   62|     23|    let idx = action.as_index();
   63|     23|    let attempts = weights.action_attempt_count[idx];
   64|     23|    let successes = weights.action_success_count[idx];
   65|       |
   66|     23|    if attempts == 0 {
   67|     17|        return 1.0;
   68|      6|    }
   69|       |
   70|       |    #[allow(clippy::cast_precision_loss)]
   71|      6|    let success_rate = successes as f32 / attempts as f32;
   72|       |
   73|       |    // Convert to modifier: 0.8-1.2 range
   74|      6|    success_rate.mul_add(0.4, 0.8)
   75|     23|}

   82|       |    #[test]
   83|      1|    fn test_manhattan_distance_overflow() {
   84|      1|        let pos1 = GridPosition { x: i32::MIN, y: 0 };
   85|      1|        let pos2 = GridPosition { x: 1, y: 0 };
   86|       |        // Original implementation panicked here: (-2147483648 - 1).abs() overflow
   87|      1|        let d = manhattan_distance(&pos1, &pos2);
   88|       |        // Correct distance is |-2147483648 - 1| = |-2147483649| = 2147483649
   89|       |        // Clamped to i32::MAX (2147483647)
   90|      1|        assert_eq!(d, i32::MAX);
   91|      1|    }

   20|      0|fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
   21|       |    // Terminal setup
   22|      0|    enable_raw_mode()?;
   23|      0|    let mut stdout = io::stdout();
   24|      0|    execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen, EnableMouseCapture)?;
   25|      0|    let backend = CrosstermBackend::new(stdout);
   26|      0|    let mut terminal = Terminal::new(backend)?;
   27|       |
   28|       |    // Run the app, ensuring cleanup happens afterwards
   29|      0|    let result = run_app(&mut terminal);
   30|       |
   31|       |    // Cleanup
   32|      0|    disable_raw_mode()?;
   33|      0|    execute!(
   34|      0|        terminal.backend_mut(),
   35|       |        LeaveAlternateScreen,
   36|       |        DisableMouseCapture
   37|      0|    )?;
   38|      0|    terminal.show_cursor()?;
   39|       |
   40|      0|    result
   41|      0|}
   42|       |
   43|      0|fn run_app(terminal: &mut Terminal<CrosstermBackend<io::Stdout>>) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
   44|      0|    let mut world = setup_world();
   45|      0|    let mut input_router = InputRouter::new();
   46|       |
   47|       |    // Main loop
   48|      0|    let tick_rate = Duration::from_millis(100); // 10 FPS base
   49|      0|    let mut last_tick = Instant::now();
   50|       |
   51|       |    loop {
   52|       |        // Input
   53|       |        // event::read() must only be called after event::poll() indicates that an event is available.
   54|       |        // The nested if structure preserves this ordering and cannot be safely collapsed.
   55|       |        #[allow(clippy::collapsible_if)]
   56|      0|        if event::poll(Duration::from_millis(10))? {
   57|      0|            match event::read()? {
   58|      0|                Event::Key(key) => {
   59|      0|                    if let Ok(game_key) = GameKeyEvent::try_from(key) {
   60|      0|                        input_router.route(&mut world, game_key);
   61|      0|                    }
   62|       |                }
   63|      0|                Event::Mouse(mouse) => {
   64|      0|                    if let Ok(game_mouse) = GameMouseEvent::try_from(mouse) {
   65|      0|                        input_router.route_mouse(&mut world, game_mouse);
   66|      0|                    }
   67|       |                }
   68|      0|                _ => {}
   69|       |            }
   70|      0|        }
   71|       |
   72|       |        // Check quit
   73|      0|        if *world.resource::<GameState>() == GameState::Quitting {
   74|      0|            break;
   75|      0|        }
   76|       |
   77|       |        // Simulation tick
   78|      0|        if last_tick.elapsed() >= tick_rate {
   79|      0|            if *world.resource::<GameState>() == GameState::Running {
   80|      0|                let speed = world.resource::<SimulationTime>().speed;
   81|      0|                if speed != SimSpeed::Paused {
   82|      0|                    run_simulation_tick(&mut world);
   83|      0|                }
   84|      0|            }
   85|       |
   86|      0|            last_tick = Instant::now();
   87|      0|        }
   88|       |
   89|       |        // Prepare render data
   90|      0|        update_render_cache(&mut world);
   91|       |
   92|       |        // Render
   93|      0|        terminal.draw(|frame| render(&world, frame))?;
   94|       |    }
   95|       |
   96|      0|    Ok(())
   97|      0|}
   98|       |

  100|       |mod tests {

  109|       |

    1|       |//! Native platform adapter (crossterm → `GameKeyEvent` / `GameMouseEvent`).
    2|       |
    3|       |use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyEventKind, MouseEvent, MouseEventKind};
    4|       |
    5|       |use super::input::{GameKeyCode, GameKeyEvent, GameMouseEvent};
    6|       |
    7|       |/// Convert a crossterm `KeyEvent` to a platform-agnostic `GameKeyEvent`.
    8|       |///
    9|       |/// Only `KeyEventKind::Press` events are translated. On Windows, crossterm
   10|       |/// also sends `Release` and `Repeat` events which would double-fire inputs.
   11|       |impl TryFrom<KeyEvent> for GameKeyEvent {
   12|       |    type Error = ();
   13|       |
   14|     15|    fn try_from(key: KeyEvent) -> Result<Self, Self::Error> {
   15|     15|        if key.kind != KeyEventKind::Press {
   16|      3|            return Err(());
   17|     12|        }
   18|     12|        let code = match key.code {
                          ^11
   19|      2|            KeyCode::Char(c) => GameKeyCode::Char(c),
   20|      1|            KeyCode::Enter => GameKeyCode::Enter,
   21|      1|            KeyCode::Esc => GameKeyCode::Esc,
   22|      1|            KeyCode::Tab => GameKeyCode::Tab,
   23|      1|            KeyCode::Up => GameKeyCode::Up,
   24|      1|            KeyCode::Down => GameKeyCode::Down,
   25|      1|            KeyCode::Left => GameKeyCode::Left,
   26|      1|            KeyCode::Right => GameKeyCode::Right,
   27|      1|            KeyCode::Backspace => GameKeyCode::Backspace,
   28|      1|            KeyCode::Delete => GameKeyCode::Delete,
   29|      1|            _ => return Err(()),
   30|       |        };
   31|     11|        Ok(Self { code })
   32|     15|    }
   33|       |}
   34|       |
   35|       |/// Convert a crossterm `MouseEvent` to a platform-agnostic `GameMouseEvent`.
   36|       |///
   37|       |/// Only left-button mouse-down events are translated; all others return `None`.
   38|       |impl TryFrom<MouseEvent> for GameMouseEvent {
   39|       |    type Error = ();
   40|       |
   41|      3|    fn try_from(mouse: MouseEvent) -> Result<Self, Self::Error> {
   42|      3|        if let MouseEventKind::Down(_button) = mouse.kind {
                                                  ^1
   43|      1|            Ok(Self {
   44|      1|                x: mouse.column,
   45|      1|                y: mouse.row,
   46|      1|            })
   47|       |        } else {
   48|      2|            Err(())
   49|       |        }
   50|      3|    }
   51|       |}

  152|       |    /// Regression test for Windows double-fire bug.
  153|       |    ///
  154|       |    /// On Windows, crossterm sends Press + Release for every keypress.
  155|       |    /// Without filtering, Space would toggle pause ON (Press) then
  156|       |    /// immediately OFF (Release), making pause appear broken.
  157|       |    ///
  158|       |    /// This test feeds raw crossterm events through the full pipeline:
  159|       |    /// crossterm KeyEvent → TryFrom → InputRouter → GameState
  160|       |    #[test]
  161|      1|    fn test_windows_press_release_does_not_double_toggle_pause() {
  162|       |        use crate::layer1::{BuildMode, DesignationMode, Viewport};
  163|       |        use crate::shared::input::{InputContext, InputContextStack, InputRouter};
  164|       |        use crate::shared::menu::MenuState;
  165|       |        use crate::shared::selection::Selection;
  166|       |        use crate::shared::state::GameState;
  167|       |        use crate::shared::time::SimulationTime;
  168|       |
  169|      1|        let mut world = bevy_ecs::prelude::World::new();
  170|      1|        world.insert_resource(GameState::Running);
  171|      1|        world.insert_resource(SimulationTime::default());
  172|      1|        world.insert_resource(Viewport::default());
  173|      1|        world.insert_resource(BuildMode::default());
  174|      1|        world.insert_resource(DesignationMode::default());
  175|      1|        world.insert_resource(MenuState::default());
  176|      1|        world.insert_resource(Selection::default());
  177|      1|        let mut stack = InputContextStack::default();
  178|      1|        stack.push(InputContext::Normal);
  179|      1|        world.insert_resource(stack);
  180|       |
  181|      1|        let mut router = InputRouter::new();
  182|       |
  183|       |        // Simulate Windows keypress: Press then Release
  184|      1|        let press = KeyEvent::new_with_kind(
  185|      1|            KeyCode::Char(' '),
  186|      1|            KeyModifiers::empty(),
  187|      1|            KeyEventKind::Press,
  188|       |        );
  189|      1|        let release = KeyEvent::new_with_kind(
  190|      1|            KeyCode::Char(' '),
  191|      1|            KeyModifiers::empty(),
  192|      1|            KeyEventKind::Release,
  193|       |        );
  194|       |
  195|       |        // Feed both events through the same path as main.rs
  196|      1|        if let Ok(game_key) = GameKeyEvent::try_from(press) {
  197|      1|            router.route(&mut world, game_key);
  198|      1|        }
                      ^0
  199|      1|        if let Ok(game_key) = GameKeyEvent::try_from(release) {
                                ^0
  200|      0|            router.route(&mut world, game_key);
  201|      1|        }
  202|       |
  203|       |        // Should be Paused — not toggled back to Running
  204|      1|        assert_eq!(
  205|      1|            *world.resource::<GameState>(),
  206|       |            GameState::Paused,
  207|      0|            "Press+Release should only toggle once (Press), not double-toggle"
  208|       |        );
  209|      1|    }
  210|       |
  211|       |    #[test]
  212|      1|    fn test_translate_mouse_down() {
  213|      1|        let mouse = MouseEvent {

   66|      5|        }

   80|       |    /// Route input to the appropriate handler based on current context.

   83|       |

   86|     12|            InputContext::Normal => handle_normal_mode(world, key),

   89|      2|            InputContext::Overlay => handle_overlay_mode(world, key),
   90|       |        }
   91|     20|    }

  116|     12|fn handle_normal_mode(world: &mut World, key: GameKeyEvent) {
  117|     12|    match key.code {
  118|      1|        GameKeyCode::Char('q') => {
  119|      1|            *world.resource_mut::<GameState>() = GameState::Quitting;
  120|      1|        }
  121|       |        GameKeyCode::Esc => {
  122|      2|            let mut selection = world.resource_mut::<Selection>();
  123|      2|            if selection.is_selected() {
  124|      1|                selection.clear();
  125|      1|            } else {
  126|      1|                *world.resource_mut::<GameState>() = GameState::Quitting;
  127|      1|            }
  128|       |        }
  129|       |        GameKeyCode::Char(' ') => {
  130|      2|            let mut state = world.resource_mut::<GameState>();
  131|      2|            *state = match *state {
  132|      2|                GameState::Running => GameState::Paused,
  133|      0|                GameState::Paused => GameState::Running,
  134|      0|                GameState::Quitting => GameState::Quitting,
  135|      0|                GameState::MainMenu => GameState::MainMenu,
  136|       |            };
  137|       |        }
  138|      1|        GameKeyCode::Char('1') => {
  139|      1|            world.resource_mut::<SimulationTime>().speed = SimSpeed::Normal;
  140|      1|        }
  141|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('2') => {
  142|      0|            world.resource_mut::<SimulationTime>().speed = SimSpeed::Fast;
  143|      0|        }
  144|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('3') => {
  145|      0|            world.resource_mut::<SimulationTime>().speed = SimSpeed::Faster;
  146|      0|        }
  147|      2|        GameKeyCode::Char('w') | GameKeyCode::Up => {
  148|      2|            let mut viewport = world.resource_mut::<Viewport>();
  149|      2|            viewport.y = viewport.y.wrapping_sub(1);
  150|      2|        }
  151|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('s') | GameKeyCode::Down => {
  152|      0|            let mut viewport = world.resource_mut::<Viewport>();
  153|      0|            viewport.y = viewport.y.wrapping_add(1);
  154|      0|        }
  155|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('a') | GameKeyCode::Left => {
  156|      0|            let mut viewport = world.resource_mut::<Viewport>();
  157|      0|            viewport.x = viewport.x.wrapping_sub(1);
  158|      0|        }
  159|      1|        GameKeyCode::Char('d') | GameKeyCode::Right => {
  160|      1|            let mut viewport = world.resource_mut::<Viewport>();
  161|      1|            viewport.x = viewport.x.wrapping_add(1);
  162|      1|        }
  163|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('b') => {
  164|      0|            // Enter build mode
  165|      0|            world
  166|      0|                .resource_mut::<InputContextStack>()
  167|      0|                .push(InputContext::BuildMode);
  168|      0|
  169|      0|            // Sync side effects
  170|      0|            let (vx, vy) = {
  171|      0|                let viewport = world.resource::<Viewport>();
  172|      0|                (viewport.x, viewport.y)
  173|      0|            };
  174|      0|            let mut build_mode = world.resource_mut::<BuildMode>();

  177|      0|                x: vx + 10,
  178|      0|                y: vy + 10,
  179|      0|            };
  180|      0|        }
  181|      1|        GameKeyCode::Char('m') => {
  182|      1|            // Enter Designation mode (Mine)
  183|      1|            enter_designation_mode(world, DesignationType::Mine);
  184|      1|        }
  185|      1|        GameKeyCode::Char('x') => {
  186|      1|            // Enter Designation mode (Demolish)
  187|      1|            enter_designation_mode(world, DesignationType::Demolish);
  188|      1|        }
  189|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('c') => {
  190|      0|            // Enter Designation mode (Chop)
  191|      0|            enter_designation_mode(world, DesignationType::Chop);
  192|      0|        }
  193|      1|        GameKeyCode::Char('l' | 'h') => {
  194|      1|            // Open chronicle
  195|      1|            world
  196|      1|                .resource_mut::<InputContextStack>()
  197|      1|                .push(InputContext::Overlay);
  198|      1|            world.resource_mut::<ChronicleUiState>().is_open = true;
  199|      1|            *world.resource_mut::<GameState>() = GameState::Paused;
  200|      1|        }
  201|      0|        _ => {}
  202|       |    }

  204|       |
  205|      2|fn handle_main_menu_mode(world: &mut World, key: GameKeyEvent) {
  206|      2|    match key.code {
  207|      0|        GameKeyCode::Up | GameKeyCode::Char('w') => {
  208|      0|            world.resource_mut::<MenuState>().prev();
  209|      0|        }
  210|      0|        GameKeyCode::Down | GameKeyCode::Char('s') => {
  211|      0|            world.resource_mut::<MenuState>().next();
  212|      0|        }

  214|      2|            let selected = world.resource::<MenuState>().selected_index;
  215|      2|            match selected {
  216|      1|                0 => {
  217|      1|                    // Start Game

  219|      1|                    let mut stack = world.resource_mut::<InputContextStack>();
  220|      1|                    stack.stack = vec![InputContext::Normal];
  221|      1|                }
  222|      1|                1 => {
  223|      1|                    // Quit
  224|      1|                    *world.resource_mut::<GameState>() = GameState::Quitting;
  225|      1|                }
  226|      0|                _ => {}
  227|       |            }
  228|       |        }
  229|      0|        GameKeyCode::Esc | GameKeyCode::Char('q') => {
  230|      0|            *world.resource_mut::<GameState>() = GameState::Quitting;
  231|      0|        }
  232|      0|        _ => {}
  233|       |    }

  265|      1|        }

  267|      0|            let mut bm = world.resource_mut::<BuildMode>();
  268|      0|            bm.cursor.y = bm.cursor.y.saturating_add(1);
  269|      0|        }
  270|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('a') | GameKeyCode::Left => {
  271|      0|            let mut bm = world.resource_mut::<BuildMode>();
  272|      0|            bm.cursor.x = bm.cursor.x.saturating_sub(1);
  273|      0|        }
  274|      1|        GameKeyCode::Char('d') | GameKeyCode::Right => {
  275|      1|            let mut bm = world.resource_mut::<BuildMode>();
  276|      1|            bm.cursor.x = bm.cursor.x.saturating_add(1);
  277|      1|        }

  283|      0|        }

  287|      0|        }
  288|      1|        _ => {}
