  292|      1|fn handle_designation_mode(world: &mut World, key: GameKeyEvent) {
  293|      1|    match key.code {
  294|      1|        GameKeyCode::Esc => {
  295|      1|            // Exit designation mode
  296|      1|            world.resource_mut::<InputContextStack>().pop();
  297|      1|            let mut mode = world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>();
  298|      1|            mode.active = false;
  299|      1|            mode.drag_start = None;
  300|      1|        }
  301|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('m') => {
  302|      0|            // Switch to Mine tool
  303|      0|            world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>().tool = DesignationType::Mine;
  304|      0|        }
  305|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('x') => {
  306|      0|            // Switch to Demolish tool
  307|      0|            world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>().tool = DesignationType::Demolish;
  308|      0|        }
  309|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('c') => {
  310|      0|            // Switch to Chop tool
  311|      0|            world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>().tool = DesignationType::Chop;
  312|      0|        }
  313|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('w') | GameKeyCode::Up => {
  314|      0|            let mut mode = world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>();
  315|      0|            mode.cursor.y = mode.cursor.y.saturating_sub(1);
  316|      0|        }
  317|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('s') | GameKeyCode::Down => {
  318|      0|            let mut mode = world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>();
  319|      0|            mode.cursor.y = mode.cursor.y.saturating_add(1);
  320|      0|        }
  321|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('a') | GameKeyCode::Left => {
  322|      0|            let mut mode = world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>();
  323|      0|            mode.cursor.x = mode.cursor.x.saturating_sub(1);
  324|      0|        }
  325|      0|        GameKeyCode::Char('d') | GameKeyCode::Right => {
  326|      0|            let mut mode = world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>();
  327|      0|            mode.cursor.x = mode.cursor.x.saturating_add(1);
  328|      0|        }
  329|       |        GameKeyCode::Char(' ') | GameKeyCode::Enter => {
  330|      0|            let mode = world.resource::<DesignationMode>();
  331|      0|            let cursor = mode.cursor;
  332|      0|            let tool = mode.tool;
  333|      0|            let drag_start = mode.drag_start;
  334|       |
  335|      0|            if let Some(start) = drag_start {
  336|      0|                // Second press: designate the rectangle and clear drag_start
  337|      0|                try_designate_area(world, start.x, start.y, cursor.x, cursor.y, tool);
  338|      0|                world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>().drag_start = None;
  339|      0|            } else {
  340|      0|                // First press: set drag_start
  341|      0|                world.resource_mut::<DesignationMode>().drag_start = Some(cursor);
  342|      0|            }
  343|       |        }
  344|      0|        GameKeyCode::Backspace | GameKeyCode::Delete => {
  345|      0|            let mode = world.resource::<DesignationMode>();
  346|      0|            try_cancel_designation(world, mode.cursor.x, mode.cursor.y);
  347|      0|        }
  348|      0|        _ => {}
  349|       |    }

  355|       |
  356|      0|    let mode = world.resource::<DesignationMode>();
  357|      0|    let tool = mode.tool;
  358|      0|    let drag_start = mode.drag_start;
  359|       |

  366|      0|            x: world_x,

  326|  5.80k|                while let Some(&next_c) = char_iter.peek() {

  328|  5.80k|                    if next_c == ']' {

  330|    661|                        break;

  332|  5.13k|                    slot_name.push(next_c);
  333|       |                }

  339|      0|                        &slot_name[0..slot_name.len() - 1]
  340|       |                    } else {
  341|    661|                        &slot_name
  342|       |                    };

  344|       |                    // Resolve slot
  345|    661|                    if let Some(val) = context.get(key) {
                                              ^522
  346|    522|                        output.push_str(val);
  347|    522|                    } else if let Some(fragment) = self.fragments.get(key) {

  348|       |                        // Pick random fragment

   48|      4|        match self {
   49|      1|            Self::Paused => 0.0,

   12|       |/// Generate pre-history events and insert them into the chronicle.

   16|       |/// 2. Borrow `&mut Chronicle` to insert events
   17|      8|pub fn generate_world_history(world: &mut World) {

   21|       |
   22|       |    // Phase 2: insert into chronicle
   23|      8|    let mut chronicle = world.resource_mut::<Chronicle>();

                       ^102  ^102
   25|    102|        chronicle.add_prehistory_event(text, importance);
   26|    102|    }

/app/src/simulation.rs:
    1|       |//! Shared simulation tick logic used by all entry points.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! Uses a Bevy `Schedule` to run all simulation systems. This enables:
    4|       |//! - Typed system params (Bevy injects `Query`, `Res`, `ResMut` automatically)
    5|       |//! - Automatic parallel execution of non-conflicting systems (with `multi_threaded`)
    6|       |//! - `par_iter_mut` for intra-system parallelism on queries
    7|       |
    8|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
    9|       |use bevy_ecs::schedule::{IntoSystemConfigs, Schedule, ScheduleLabel};
   10|       |
   11|       |use crate::experimental::biography::biography_monitor_system;
   12|       |use crate::experimental::dreams::dream_system;
   13|       |use crate::gpu::evaluate::gpu_evaluate_actions;
   14|       |use crate::layer1::{
   15|       |    advance_season_system, arrival_handler_system, check_milestones_system,
   16|       |    clean_dead_residents_system, clean_dead_workers_system, cleanup_previous_assignment_system,
   17|       |    clothing_wear_system, consume_food_system, death_system, decay_needs_system,
   18|       |    fire_damage_pops_system, fire_damage_system, fire_spread_system, haul_system,
   19|       |    hypothermia_system, memory_decay_system, movement_system, notification_expiration_system,
   20|       |    process_refining_system, process_research_system, process_scan_system,
   21|       |    process_start_plan_system, produce_food_system, restore_leisure_system,
   22|       |    restore_rest_in_housing_system, spoilage_system, starvation_damage_system,
   23|       |    track_plan_outcomes_system, update_action_timer_system, update_resource_caps_system,
   24|       |    work_execution_system,
   25|       |};
   26|       |use crate::shared::time::SimulationTime;
   27|       |
   28|       |/// Schedule label for the main simulation tick.
   29|       |#[derive(ScheduleLabel, Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
   30|       |pub struct SimulationSchedule;
   31|       |
   32|       |/// Build the simulation schedule with all systems and ordering constraints.
   33|       |///
   34|       |/// Systems are organized into ordered groups matching the original sequential execution:
   35|       |///
   36|       |/// ```text
   37|       |/// 1. AI Decision:     gpu_evaluate_actions → update_action_timer
   38|       |/// 2. Execution:       cleanup_previous → process_start_plan → movement → arrival → work/haul
   39|       |/// 3. Economy:         update_resource_caps, advance_season, produce_food, process_refining,
   40|       |///                     process_research, restore_rest, restore_leisure (can run in parallel)
   41|       |/// 4. Consumption:     consume_food → decay_needs → kill_starving → clean_dead_*
   42|       |/// 5. Observation:     track_plan_outcomes, biography, dreams, milestones (can run in parallel)
   43|       |/// 6. Tick increment:  (handled outside schedule)
   44|       |/// ```
   45|       |#[must_use]
   46|      4|pub fn build_simulation_schedule() -> Schedule {
   47|      4|    let mut schedule = Schedule::new(SimulationSchedule);
   48|       |
   49|       |    // --- AI Decision Chain (GPU compute) ---
   50|      4|    schedule.add_systems((
   51|      4|        gpu_evaluate_actions,
   52|      4|        update_action_timer_system.after(gpu_evaluate_actions),
   53|      4|    ));
   54|       |
   55|       |    // --- Execution Chain (must be sequential) ---
   56|      4|    schedule.add_systems((
   57|      4|        cleanup_previous_assignment_system.after(update_action_timer_system),
   58|      4|        process_start_plan_system.after(cleanup_previous_assignment_system),
   59|      4|        movement_system.after(process_start_plan_system),
   60|      4|        arrival_handler_system.after(movement_system),
   61|      4|        work_execution_system.after(arrival_handler_system),
   62|      4|        haul_system.after(arrival_handler_system),
   63|      4|        process_scan_system.after(arrival_handler_system),
   64|      4|    ));
   65|       |
   66|       |    // --- Economy (after execution, before consumption) ---
   67|       |    // These systems can run in parallel with each other.
   68|      4|    schedule.add_systems((
   69|      4|        update_resource_caps_system.after(work_execution_system),
   70|      4|        advance_season_system.after(work_execution_system),
   71|      4|        produce_food_system.after(work_execution_system),
   72|      4|        process_refining_system.after(work_execution_system),
   73|      4|        process_research_system.after(work_execution_system),
   74|      4|        restore_rest_in_housing_system.after(work_execution_system),
   75|      4|        restore_leisure_system.after(work_execution_system),
   76|      4|    ));
   77|       |
   78|       |    // --- Environment (Fire) ---
   79|      4|    schedule.add_systems((
   80|      4|        fire_spread_system.after(work_execution_system),
   81|      4|        fire_damage_pops_system.after(fire_spread_system),
   82|      4|        crate::layer1::structure::fire_damage_structure_system.after(fire_spread_system),
   83|      4|        fire_damage_system
   84|      4|            .after(fire_spread_system)
   85|      4|            .after(fire_damage_pops_system)
   86|      4|            .after(crate::layer1::structure::fire_damage_structure_system),
   87|      4|    ));
   88|       |
   89|       |    // --- Consumption Chain (sequential, depends on economy) ---
   90|      4|    schedule.add_systems((
   91|      4|        consume_food_system
   92|      4|            .after(produce_food_system)
   93|      4|            .after(update_resource_caps_system),
   94|      4|        clothing_wear_system.after(consume_food_system),
   95|      4|        spoilage_system.after(consume_food_system),
   96|      4|        decay_needs_system.after(consume_food_system),
   97|      4|        memory_decay_system.after(decay_needs_system),
   98|      4|        notification_expiration_system.after(decay_needs_system),
   99|      4|        hypothermia_system.after(decay_needs_system),
  100|      4|        starvation_damage_system.after(decay_needs_system),
  101|      4|        death_system
  102|      4|            .after(starvation_damage_system)
  103|      4|            .after(hypothermia_system),
  104|      4|        clean_dead_residents_system.after(death_system),
  105|      4|        clean_dead_workers_system.after(death_system),
  106|      4|    ));
  107|       |
  108|       |    // --- Observation (after consumption, can run in parallel) ---
  109|      4|    schedule.add_systems((
  110|      4|        track_plan_outcomes_system.after(death_system),
  111|      4|        biography_monitor_system.after(death_system),
  112|      4|        dream_system.after(death_system),
  113|      4|        check_milestones_system.after(death_system),
  114|      4|    ));
  115|       |
  116|      4|    schedule
  117|      4|}
  118|       |
  119|       |/// Run one simulation tick: all game systems via schedule, then increment tick counter.
  120|     11|pub fn run_simulation_tick(world: &mut World) {
  121|       |    // Initialize schedule on first call (stored in World's Schedules resource)
  122|     11|    if !world.contains_resource::<Schedules>() {
  123|      2|        world.insert_resource(Schedules::default());
  124|      9|    }
  125|       |
  126|       |    // Add our schedule if not yet added
  127|       |    {
  128|     11|        let schedules = world.resource::<Schedules>();
  129|     11|        if schedules.get(SimulationSchedule).is_none() {
  130|      2|            let schedule = build_simulation_schedule();
  131|      2|            world.add_schedule(schedule);
  132|      9|        }
  133|       |    }
  134|       |
  135|     11|    world.run_schedule(SimulationSchedule);
  136|     11|    world.resource_mut::<SimulationTime>().tick += 1;
  137|     11|}

  106|      0|pub const fn get_importance_color(importance: EventImportance) -> Color {

  108|      0|        EventImportance::Legendary => Color::Yellow,

   16|       |/// Represents a renderable entity on the map.
   17|       |///
   18|       |/// This enum abstracts the different types of entities that can appear on the map grid.
   19|       |/// It carries just enough data to determine the character and color to render.
   20|       |#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
   21|       |pub enum RenderEntity {
   22|       |    /// A player designation (e.g., [`DesignationType::Mine`], [`DesignationType::Chop`]).
   23|       |    Designation(DesignationType),
   24|       |    /// A constructed building (e.g., [`BuildingType::Farm`], [`BuildingType::Housing`]).
   25|       |    Building(BuildingType),
   26|       |    /// A colonist ([`crate::layer1::pop::Pop`]), carrying a display character and color based on status.
   27|       |    Pop(&'static str, Color),
   28|       |    /// An anomaly scan target (e.g., Ruins, Flora).
   29|       |    Anomaly(AnomalyType),
   30|       |    /// A loose resource item on the ground (e.g., [`ResourceType::Wood`]).
   31|       |    Item(ResourceType),
   32|       |}
   33|       |
   34|       |impl RenderEntity {
   35|       |    /// Returns the rendering priority (higher is drawn on top).
   36|       |    ///
   37|       |    /// The z-ordering is:
   38|       |    /// 1. **Designations** (Top): Overlays like "Mine" need to be visible over everything.
   39|       |    /// 2. **Buildings**: Walls and structures cover pops.
   40|       |    /// 3. **Pops**: Colonists move around on the ground.
   41|       |    /// 4. **Anomalies**: Special sites (under pops).
   42|       |    /// 5. **Items** (Bottom): Resources sit on the floor.
   43|       |    ///
   44|       |    /// # Examples
   45|       |    ///
   46|       |    /// ```
   47|       |    /// use scale::ui::map::RenderEntity;
   48|       |    /// use scale::layer1::DesignationType;
   49|       |    /// use ratatui::style::Color;
   50|       |    ///
   51|       |    /// let des = RenderEntity::Designation(DesignationType::Mine);
   52|       |    /// let pop = RenderEntity::Pop("☺", Color::Yellow);
   53|       |    ///
   54|       |    /// assert!(des.priority() > pop.priority());
   55|       |    /// ```
   56|       |    #[must_use]
   57|      0|    pub const fn priority(&self) -> u8 {
   58|      0|        match self {
   59|      0|            Self::Designation(_) => 5,
   60|      0|            Self::Building(_) => 4,
   61|      0|            Self::Pop(_, _) => 3,
   62|      0|            Self::Anomaly(_) => 2,
   63|      0|            Self::Item(_) => 1,
   64|       |        }
   65|      0|    }
   66|       |}
   67|       |
   68|       |/// Cache for renderable entities to avoid repeated allocations and iterations.
   69|       |///
   70|       |/// Rendering the map requires determining what is at each (x, y) coordinate.
   71|       |/// Iterating the entire ECS world for every tile would be O(N * W * H), which is too slow.
   72|       |///
   73|       |/// Instead, we iterate the ECS *once* per frame and populate this hash map:
   74|       |/// `GridPosition -> RenderEntity`.
   75|       |///
   76|       |/// This reduces the per-tile lookup to O(1), making rendering O(W * H).
   77|       |#[derive(Resource, Default)]
   78|       |pub struct RenderCache {
   79|       |    /// Cached entity data.
   80|       |    pub entities: HashMap<GridPosition, RenderEntity>,
   81|       |}
   82|       |
   83|       |/// Updates the [`RenderCache`] by iterating the world once.
   84|       |///
   85|       |/// This system must run before rendering to ensure the cache reflects the latest frame state.
   86|       |/// It respects the priority defined in [`RenderEntity::priority()`].
   87|      0|pub fn update_render_cache(world: &mut World) {
   88|      0|    let mut cache = world.remove_resource::<RenderCache>().unwrap_or_default();
   89|       |
   90|      0|    cache.entities.clear();
   91|       |
   92|      0|    for e in world.iter_entities() {
   93|      0|        if let Some(pos) = e.get::<GridPosition>() {
   94|       |            // Check for Designation
   95|      0|            if let Some(designation) = e.get::<Designation>() {
   96|      0|                insert_if_higher_priority(
   97|      0|                    &mut cache.entities,
   98|      0|                    *pos,
   99|      0|                    RenderEntity::Designation(designation.designation_type),
  100|      0|                );
  101|      0|            }
  102|       |
  103|       |            // Check for Building
  104|      0|            if let Some(building) = e.get::<Building>() {
  105|      0|                insert_if_higher_priority(
  106|      0|                    &mut cache.entities,
  107|      0|                    *pos,
  108|      0|                    RenderEntity::Building(building.building_type),
  109|      0|                );
  110|      0|            }
  111|       |
  112|       |            // Check for Pop (via Needs)
  113|      0|            if let Some(needs) = e.get::<Needs>() {
  114|      0|                let (text, color) = get_pop_display(needs);
  115|      0|                insert_if_higher_priority(
  116|      0|                    &mut cache.entities,
  117|      0|                    *pos,
  118|      0|                    RenderEntity::Pop(text, color),
  119|      0|                );
  120|      0|            }
  121|       |
  122|       |            // Check for Anomaly
  123|      0|            if let Some(anomaly) = e.get::<Anomaly>() {
  124|      0|                insert_if_higher_priority(
  125|      0|                    &mut cache.entities,
  126|      0|                    *pos,
  127|      0|                    RenderEntity::Anomaly(anomaly.anomaly_type),
  128|      0|                );
  129|      0|            }
  130|       |
  131|       |            // Check for ResourceItem
  132|      0|            if let Some(item) = e.get::<ResourceItem>() {
  133|      0|                insert_if_higher_priority(
  134|      0|                    &mut cache.entities,
  135|      0|                    *pos,
  136|      0|                    RenderEntity::Item(item.resource_type),
  137|      0|                );
  138|      0|            }
  139|      0|        }
  140|       |    }
  141|       |
  142|      0|    world.insert_resource(cache);
  143|      0|}
  144|       |
  145|      0|fn insert_if_higher_priority(
  146|      0|    map: &mut HashMap<GridPosition, RenderEntity>,
  147|      0|    pos: GridPosition,
  148|      0|    entity: RenderEntity,
  149|      0|) {
  150|      0|    map.entry(pos)
  151|      0|        .and_modify(|e| {
  152|      0|            if entity.priority() > e.priority() {
  153|      0|                *e = entity;
  154|      0|            }
  155|      0|        })
  156|      0|        .or_insert(entity);

  224|       |/// Builds a vector of text lines to render the map layer (terrain, pops, buildings, cursor).
  225|       |///
  226|       |/// This is the core rendering logic for the map view. It iterates screen coordinates
  227|       |/// and resolves the character/color for each cell based on:
  228|       |/// 1. Active cursors (Build/Designate).
  229|       |/// 2. Drag selection rectangles.
  230|       |/// 3. Cached entities ([`RenderCache`]).
  231|       |/// 4. Base terrain.

  233|       |#[allow(clippy::too_many_lines)]
  234|      0|pub fn build_map_layer_spans<S: BuildHasher>(ctx: MapRenderContext<'_, S>) -> Vec<Line<'static>> {
  235|      0|    let mut lines: Vec<Line> = Vec::with_capacity(ctx.area.height as usize);
  236|       |

  238|      0|        let world_y = ctx.viewport.y + i32::from(screen_y);
  239|      0|        let mut line_spans = Vec::with_capacity(ctx.area.width as usize);
  240|       |
  241|      0|        for screen_x in 0..ctx.area.width {
  242|      0|            let world_x = ctx.viewport.x + i32::from(screen_x);
  243|       |
  244|       |            // Build mode cursor (highest priority)
  245|      0|            if let Some((_, selected, can_place)) = ctx
  246|      0|                .build_mode
  247|      0|                .filter(|(cursor, _, _)| cursor.x == world_x && cursor.y == world_y)

  249|      0|                let bg = if can_place { Color::Green } else { Color::Red };
  250|      0|                let text = get_building_char(selected);
  251|      0|                line_spans.push(Span::styled(
  252|      0|                    text.to_string(),
  253|      0|                    Style::default().fg(Color::White).bg(bg),
  254|       |                ));
  255|      0|                continue;
  256|      0|            }
  257|       |
  258|       |            // Designation mode cursor (highest priority, shared with build mode)
  259|      0|            if let Some((cursor, selected, can_place, drag_start)) = ctx.designation_mode {
  260|      0|                if cursor.x == world_x && cursor.y == world_y {
  261|      0|                    let bg = if can_place { Color::Green } else { Color::Red };
  262|      0|                    let text = get_designation_char(selected);
  263|      0|                    line_spans.push(Span::styled(text, Style::default().fg(Color::White).bg(bg)));
  264|      0|                    continue;

  281|      0|                                let color = seasonal_gfx::get_texture_override(tile, ctx.season)
  282|      0|                                    .unwrap_or_else(|| get_terrain_color(tile));
  283|      0|                                (get_terrain_char(tile), color)
  284|      0|                            })

  291|       |                        ));

  293|      0|                    }

  613|       |
  614|       |const HEALTHY_THRESHOLD: f32 = 0.6;
  615|       |const WARNING_THRESHOLD: f32 = 0.3;
  616|       |
  617|       |/// Returns the character and color for rendering a pop.
  618|       |///
  619|       |/// Display is driven primarily by hunger (the only lethal need).
  620|       |/// Leisure/rest affect mood but not survival, so they only downgrade
  621|       |/// from happy to neutral — never to the "dying" indicator.
  622|       |///
  623|       |/// # Examples
  624|       |///
  625|       |/// ```
  626|       |/// use scale::ui::map::get_pop_display;
  627|       |/// use scale::layer1::Needs;
  628|       |/// use ratatui::style::Color;
  629|       |///
  630|       |/// let happy = Needs { hunger: 1.0, rest: 1.0, leisure: 1.0 };
  631|       |/// let (char, color) = get_pop_display(&happy);
  632|       |/// assert_eq!(char, "☺");
  633|       |/// assert_eq!(color, Color::Yellow);
  634|       |/// ```
  635|       |#[must_use]
  636|      0|pub fn get_pop_display(needs: &Needs) -> (&'static str, Color) {
  637|      0|    if needs.hunger > HEALTHY_THRESHOLD {
  638|       |        // Well-fed: check other needs for mood
  639|      0|        if needs.worst() > WARNING_THRESHOLD {
  640|      0|            ("☺", Color::Yellow)
  641|       |        } else {
  642|      0|            ("☻", Color::Rgb(255, 165, 0)) // fed but tired/bored
  643|       |        }
  644|      0|    } else if needs.hunger > WARNING_THRESHOLD {
  645|      0|        ("☻", Color::Rgb(255, 165, 0)) // getting hungry
  646|       |    } else {
  647|      0|        ("☹", Color::Red) // starving
  648|       |    }
  649|      0|}

/app/src/ui/mod.rs:
    1|       |//! User Interface.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! This module handles the TUI rendering using `ratatui`.
    4|       |//!
    5|       |//! # Architecture
    6|       |//!
    7|       |//! The UI is built using the `ratatui` crate, which provides a backend-agnostic way to
    8|       |//! render terminal interfaces. This allows the game to run:
    9|       |//! - **Natively** using `crossterm`.
   10|       |//! - **In Browser** using `ratzilla` (WASM).
   11|       |//!
   12|       |//! # Layout
   13|       |//!
   14|       |//! The `render` function acts as the main entry point for the UI system. It handles
   15|       |//! the high-level layout switching:
   16|       |//!
   17|       |//! 1. **Main Menu**: If `GameState` is `MainMenu`, it delegates to `render_main_menu`.
   18|       |//! 2. **Game Interface**: Otherwise, it renders the simulation view, split into:
   19|       |//!    - **Map**: The main gameplay area (`map::render_map`).
   20|       |//!    - **Info Panel**: Selected entity details (`panels::render_info_panel`).
   21|       |//!    - **Status Bar**: Global colony stats (`status::render_status_bar`).
   22|       |//!    - **Chronicle**: Historical events overlay (`chronicle::render_chronicle`).
   23|       |
   24|       |/// Chronicle overlay rendering.
   25|       |pub mod chronicle;
   26|       |/// Entity inspector panel.
   27|       |pub mod inspector;
   28|       |/// Map rendering logic.
   29|       |pub mod map;
   30|       |/// Main menu rendering.
   31|       |pub mod menu;
   32|       |/// Info panels (inspector, etc).
   33|       |pub mod panels;
   34|       |/// Status bar rendering.
   35|       |pub mod status;
   36|       |
   37|       |use bevy_ecs::prelude::*;
   38|       |use ratatui::prelude::*;
   39|       |
   40|       |use crate::shared::menu::MenuState;
   41|       |use crate::shared::state::GameState;
   42|       |
   43|       |use self::chronicle::render_chronicle;
   44|       |use self::map::render_map;
   45|       |use self::menu::render_main_menu;
   46|       |use self::panels::render_info_panel;
   47|       |use self::status::render_status_bar;
   48|       |
   49|       |/// Render the full game UI for one frame.
   50|       |///
   51|       |/// This function is called every frame by the platform layer (native main loop or WASM animation frame).
   52|       |/// It queries the ECS world for necessary state (`GameState`, `MenuState`, etc.) and draws to the
   53|       |/// provided `ratatui` frame.
   54|       |///
   55|       |/// # Arguments
   56|       |///
   57|       |/// * `world` - The ECS world containing all game state and resources.
   58|       |/// * `frame` - The `ratatui` frame to render into.
   59|      0|pub fn render(world: &World, frame: &mut Frame) {
   60|      0|    if *world.resource::<GameState>() == GameState::MainMenu {
   61|      0|        let menu_state = world.resource::<MenuState>();
   62|      0|        render_main_menu(frame, frame.area(), menu_state);
   63|      0|        return;
   64|      0|    }
   65|       |
   66|       |    // Main vertical split: content + status bar
   67|      0|    let main_chunks = Layout::default()
   68|      0|        .direction(Direction::Vertical)
   69|      0|        .constraints([
   70|      0|            Constraint::Min(10),   // Content area
   71|      0|            Constraint::Length(1), // Status bar
   72|      0|        ])
   73|      0|        .split(frame.area());
   74|       |
   75|       |    // Horizontal split: map + info panel
   76|      0|    let content_chunks = Layout::default()
   77|      0|        .direction(Direction::Horizontal)
   78|      0|        .constraints([
   79|      0|            Constraint::Min(20),    // Map area
   80|      0|            Constraint::Length(20), // Info panel
   81|      0|        ])
   82|      0|        .split(main_chunks[0]);
   83|       |
   84|      0|    let map_area = content_chunks[0];
   85|      0|    let info_area = content_chunks[1];
   86|      0|    let status_area = main_chunks[1];
   87|       |
   88|       |    // Render map
   89|      0|    render_map(frame, map_area, world);
   90|       |
   91|       |    // Render info panel
   92|      0|    render_info_panel(frame, info_area, world);
   93|       |
   94|       |    // Render status bar
   95|      0|    render_status_bar(frame, status_area, world);
   96|       |
   97|       |    // Render chronicle
   98|      0|    render_chronicle(frame, frame.area(), world);
   99|      0|}

  254|       |/// Helper to get the status string for headless testing or assertions.
  255|       |///
  256|       |/// Wraps [`get_status_line`] and converts the `Line` to a plain `String`.
  257|       |///
  258|       |/// # Examples
  259|       |///
  260|       |/// ```
  261|       |/// use scale::ui::status::get_status_string;
  262|       |/// use scale::shared::time::SimSpeed;
  263|       |/// use scale::layer1::{BuildMode, DesignationMode};
  264|       |///
  265|       |/// let status = get_status_string(
  266|       |///     10,                 // tick
  267|       |///     SimSpeed::Normal,   // speed
  268|       |///     false,              // paused
  269|       |///     &BuildMode::default(),
  270|       |///     &DesignationMode::default(),
  271|       |///     Some("Outpost"),    // location
  272|       |///     5,                  // pop count
  273|       |///     100.0,              // food
  274|       |///     10.0,               // tools
  275|       |///     0.8                 // morale
  276|       |/// );
  277|       |///
  278|       |/// assert!(status.contains("Day 10"));
  279|       |/// assert!(status.contains("Souls: 5"));
  280|       |/// assert!(status.contains("Outpost"));
  281|       |/// ```
  282|       |#[must_use]
  283|       |#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  284|      2|pub fn get_status_string(
  285|      2|    tick: u64,
  286|      2|    speed: SimSpeed,
  287|      2|    paused: bool,
  288|      2|    build_mode: &BuildMode,
  289|      2|    designation_mode: &DesignationMode,
  290|      2|    location_name: Option<&str>,
  291|      2|    pop_count: usize,
  292|      2|    food_yield: f32,
  293|      2|    tools: f32,
  294|      2|    morale: f32,
  295|      2|) -> String {
  296|      2|    let line = get_status_line(
  297|      2|        tick,
  298|      2|        speed,
  299|      2|        paused,
  300|      2|        build_mode,
  301|      2|        designation_mode,
  302|      2|        location_name,
  303|      2|        pop_count,
  304|      2|        food_yield,
  305|      2|        tools,
  306|      2|        morale,
  307|       |    );
  308|       |
  309|      2|    line.spans
  310|      2|        .iter()
  311|     30|        .map(|s| s.content.as_ref())
                       ^2
  312|      2|        .collect::<String>()

  370|      1|    fn test_render_status_bar_calculates_morale() {

  380|       |        // Setup resources needed by render_status_bar

  407|       |

  411|      1|        memories.add(MemoryType::WitnessedDeath, 0);
  412|      1|        world.spawn((

  419|      1|            memories,
  420|      1|        ));
  421|       |

  423|       |

  425|      1|        let mut terminal = Terminal::new(backend).unwrap();
  426|       |

  432|       |
  433|       |        // Convert buffer to string to check content

  435|      1|        let cells: Vec<String> = buffer
  436|      1|            .content
  437|      1|            .iter()
  438|    100|            .map(|c| c.symbol().to_string())
                           ^1
  439|      1|            .collect();

  441|       |
  442|      1|        assert!(full_text.contains("Morale: 60%"));
